2 유럽 GAC supplies 115 Aion EVs for Grab’s ride-hailing fleet in Thailand
GAC supplies 115 Aion EVs for Grab’s ride-hailing fleet in Thailand
2026-08-19
GAC has delivered 115 Aion EVs for use on Grab's ride-hailing platform in Thailand through two separate partnerships: 100 units under a tie-up with CLVG and ICBC (Thai) Leasing, and 15 units through a collaboration with Siam Taxi Cooperative. It recently held separate handover ceremonies of these EVs in Bangkok.
CLVG, a local car rental and mobility solutions provider, has acquired the 100 EVs from GAC and leased them to Grab drivers. ICBC (Thai) Leasing, a subsidiary of Industrial and Commercial Bank of China (Thai) and part of the broader ICBC Group, is providing the drivers with financing support for the lease.
These 100 EVs are part of a broader agreement between GAC and CLVG to supply 2,000 EVs for ride-hailing services in Thailand. When the deal was first announcedlast year, CLVG also received the first batch of 100 EVs – made up entirely of Aion Y Plus units.
Images and videos from the handover event suggest GAC has now supplied a mix of two models: Aion UT and Aion Y Plus. The company assembles both EVs locally at its factory in Amata City Industrial Estate in Rayong Province, where it rolled out its 10,000th vehicle last month. For retail buyers, it has priced the Aion UT at 519,900 baht (~€13,600) or 619,900 baht (~€16,200), depending on the trim, and the Aion Y Plus at 749,000 baht (~€19,600).
The 15 EVs GAC supplied to Siam Taxi Cooperative comprise the Aion ES and Aion Y Plus. Images from the handover ceremony show that at least nine units are of the former, an electric sedan priced at 846,000 baht (~€22,100) which is also assembled locally. GAC has positioned this model as a competitor to the Toyota Corolla, one of Thailand’s most popular ICE taxi models. It plans to deliver 85 more EVs to Siam Taxi Cooperative within three months, bringing the total fleet size under the partnership to 100 vehicles.
In the first half of 2026, GAC recorded 1,416 electric taxi registrations in Thailand, which gave it a market share of over 61 per cent. The Chinese automaker is taking new measures to strengthen its position in the segment. On Tuesday, the company launched a ‘Taxi Easy’ campaign with electric taxi rental provider EV7, under which qualifying drivers who want to adopt electric taxis may receive a subsidy of up to 5,000 baht (€131) to cover their initial expenses.
gacgroup.com(Siam Taxi),gacgroup.com(CVLG) (links in Thai)

3 유럽 Ebusco secures six million euros from shareholder
Ebusco secures six million euros from shareholder
2026-08-19
Troubled e-bus manufacturer Ebusco has received six million euros from its existing shareholder Rhein Holding. At the end of June, the company had only €2.1 million in liquid funds left. In parallel, Ebusco is working on securing financing of around €30 million.
During its half-year results presentation last Friday, Ebusco announced it was negotiating a short-term liquidity solution with one of its existing shareholders – which has now been confirmed to be Rhein Holding B.V. It has committed to investing a total of six million euros in equity in Ebusco, split into two tranches of three million euros each. The first tranche is expected to be disbursed in the coming days, with the second due before the end of August.
In return for the investment, Ebusco will issue 31,725,049 new ordinary shares at a price of €0.189125 per share, representing a discount of approximately 15 per cent on the closing price of €0.2225 on 14 August. Rhein Holding currently holds around 5.1 per cent of Ebusco, and following the completion of the capital increase, its stake is expected to rise to approximately 16.6 per cent. This will also grant Rhein Holding the right to nominate a member to the Ebusco supervisory board, though the appointment must still be confirmed by shareholders at a general meeting. The shares are subject to a 90-day lock-up period.
The six million euros comes at a critical time for Ebusco. While the Dutch electric bus manufacturer managed tosignificantly reduce its losses in the first half of the yearand achieve a positive gross margin for the first time since 2022, its financial situation remains precarious. Liquid funds decreased from €7.4 million to just €2.1 million; net losses stood at €24.9 million, while equity was at minus €14.8 million. The operational impact of this is clear: in the first half of the year, Ebusco delivered only 16 buses, compared to 47 in the same period last year. Some outstanding deliveries have already been postponed to the first quarter of 2027.
The capital increase now agreed is therefore only part of a larger financing solution. In parallel, Ebusco continues to work on a letter of credit facility worth around €30 million with an Asian partner, secured by a guarantee from Chinese battery manufacturer and Ebusco shareholder Gotion. However, final documentation remains pending.
Regardless of these short-term financing measures, the restructuring process continues. Ebusco has largely completed its transformation from a traditional vehicle manufacturer to an Original Equipment Designer (OED). Assembly is increasingly being outsourced to contract manufacturers in Asia, while the company focuses on development, final assembly, and customer service. At the same time, Ebusco continues to seek astrategic solution for its bus business. Options include the sale of a controlling stake or the formation of a joint venture. However, no binding agreement has been reached thus far.

4 유럽 Voltempo unveils ‘cabless, driverless’ electric truck
Voltempo unveils ‘cabless, driverless’ electric truck
2026-08-19
Voltempo is developing a 15.6-meter-long cabless electric truck. The autonomous vehicle aims to offer increased loading volume and payload capacity. An initial prototype is planned for 2027.
Voltempo describes its concept as a “Smart Trailer”. The vehicle will feature a continuous, rigid structure and, at 15.6 metres in length, will significantly exceed the current UK maximum of twelve metres permitted for lorries with a fixed chassis.Autocarreports it is designed with five axles and a permissible gross weight of 42 tonnes.
The prototype is being built by Berkeley Coachworks, while the electric powertrain will be supplied by a major lorry manufacturer that has not yet been named. No further details about the battery, range, or charging capacity have been released so far. The prototype is scheduled for completion by September 2027, after which tests are planned on a route designated for autonomous vehicles in South Wales. Public road trials may follow at a later stage.
Voltempo states that production could begin as early as late 2027, with deliveries expected by mid-2028. Initially, the vehicles will be manufactured in the UK, with production expanding to mainland Europe later. The company sees fixed logistics routes as the primary application for the vehicle. In addition to increased transport capacity, autonomous operation is intended to help address the driver shortage.
Voltempo is not alone in this space. The Swedish startup Einride has also developed an autonomous electric lorry, theGen 2 Rigid Large, which has since been renamed the eBot. However, its dimensions and weight remain undisclosed. While the eBot is still a prototype, Einride has primarily generated revenue by supplying conventional electric lorries to logistics companies. Recently,DAF and Einrideannounced plans to jointly develop an autonomous electric lorry for commercial use to achieveSAE Level 4autonomy. Initial tests of the interfaces between Einride’s driving system and DAF’s electric lorry platform are scheduled to begin later in 2026.

5 유럽 Einride procures 500 Tesla Semi trucks
Einride procures 500 Tesla Semi trucks
2026-08-19
Einride, a provider specialising in electric and autonomous freight transport, wants to expand its electric fleet in the USA with 500 Tesla Semi trucks. The battery-powered trucks are to be used, among other things, for Amazon's logistics operations.
Einride has announced a partnership with Tesla to prepare for the “world’s largest Tesla Semi deployment”. The model in question is Tesla’s battery-electric truck, which is set to be introduced in the US later this year following the launch of series productionthis springat Gigafactory Nevada. Einride specifically announced that it will deploy “500 Tesla Semi trucks on its Saga AI platform for Amazon and other customers, converting signed contracts into revenue-generating freight capacity”.
Einride has not specified how the vehicles will be added to its fleet. The Swedish company, which is listed on the US stock exchange, refers to a newly agreed collaboration with Tesla. Whether the vehicles will be purchased, leased, or acquired through another type of agreement remains unclear. However, the scale of the procurement is evident: the 500 Semi units will expand Einride’s battery-electric truck fleet in the USA to 750 vehicles. This procurement will thus account for two-thirds of the company’s future electric fleet.
Regarding future deployment of the Semi fleet, Einride states that the electric trucks will operate across North America for its customers – with Amazon the only to be named explicitly. Specifically, new electric freight corridors are planned in California, Texas, New Jersey, Illinois, and Georgia. According to an accompanying statement, the procurement of the 500 units is set to begin in September 2026 and will be completed within two years. The financing is fully covered by third-party solutions, though Einride has not provided further details.
For context: Einride was founded in 2016 and is headquartered in Stockholm. The Swedish company initially aimed to deploy driverless commercial vehicles and has already developed a corresponding concept vehicle called theT-Pod. In recent years, however, the company’s core product has been the Saga AI software platform, which acts as an intelligent operating system for electric (and future autonomous) vehicle fleets, as well as charging and connectivity networks. This platform is designed to enable companies to transport goods in a more environmentally friendly and cost-effective way.
“As electric trucks introduce operating variables, such as charging time and energy costs that shift situationally, they require a sophisticated logistics approach to execute,” Einride stated in an announcement. Against this backdrop, Saga AI optimises a fleet of individual electric trucks into a coordinated network. To date, Saga AI has enabled over 19 million electric miles and 42,000 optimisation sessions worldwide.
“This deployment is yet another proof point that we can execute at the scale our customers demand,” says Roozbeh Charli, Chief Executive Officer of Einride. “Working closely with Tesla to bring next-generation Semis into active operations quickly and at scale is a testament to the strength of that partnership, and how quickly this technology is maturing from promise to daily operations.”
“Einride is at the forefront of sustainable freight and we are thrilled to deepen our relationship with them through this order of 500 Semis,” responds Dan Priestley, Director of Semi at Tesla. “EV heavy trucks provide lower costs per mile from fuel savings, reduced maintenance, and better uptime over diesel trucks. These savings increase further through operational efficiency when deploying EV trucks at scale, and we are excited that Einride recognizes this and look forward to supporting their deployments.”
The partners also emphasised that customers will gain “earlier access” to the Tesla Semi through Einride. The electric truck is manufactured in a dedicated facility adjacent to Gigafactory Nevada. AsElectrekreported at the start of production in April, the plant is designed for an annual capacity of 50,000 trucks, though production will be ramped up gradually – with immediate output at this level not expected. Nevertheless, the start of series production marks a decisive moment for Tesla. The economies of scale from mass production are intended to reduce the Semi’s price to $290,000 and $260,000 for the Long-Range and Standard versions, respectively. At these prices, the model is set to become the most cost-effective Class 8 battery-electric tractor on the US market, according to industry experts. At the same time, the Semi outperforms some of its ageing competitors in terms of technical specifications.
Today, a few Tesla Semi trucks are already seen sporadically on US roads. These initial units were largely hand-built on a pilot line. Most of these pre-production vehicles are believed to be part of Tesla’s own fleet, with an additional 36 units confirmed in PepsiCo’s California fleet. Teslabegan production of the first pre-production Semi units in the USA in October 2022. In the three years that followed, Tesla focused on “refining the design, cutting roughly 1,000 lbs from the truck, and building out a dedicated factory adjacent to Gigafactory Nevada in Sparks,”according toElectrek. In February, the manufacturer released the final production specifications, confirming two variants: a Standard Range with 325 miles of range (~523 km) and a gross vehicle weight of 82,000 pounds (around 37.2 tonnes), and a Long Range with 500 miles of range (~800 km).
Both variants feature a powertrain with three integrated electric motors delivering up to 800 kW of power, as well asbatteries with capacities of 548 kWh and 822 kWh.Further specifications can be found here. Another key point: the Semi is designed to support megawatt charging at 1.2 MW. Corresponding charging infrastructure is currently being developed in parts of the USA.
The charging hardware to be installed is Tesla’s1.2-MW charging solution, announced last sp

6 유럽 Daimler Truck Completes Secondary Offering of ARCHION Shares
Daimler Truck Completes Secondary Offering of ARCHION Shares
2026-08-19
Daimler Truck completes secondary offering of shares in ARCHION Corporation (“ARCHION”); equity stake reduced to 25% as planned, matching Toyota Motor Corporation (“Toyota”)’s equity stake level and completing the planned post-integration ownership structure in ARCHION.
As part of the secondary offering, Daimler Truck generated cash proceeds of approximately €600 million.
Broader shareholder base with high-quality investors…
Leinfelden-Echterdingen – The Daimler Truck Group (“Daimler Truck”) has successfully completed the previously announced secondary offering of shares of ARCHION. Following settlement of the transaction, Daimler Truck's equity stake in ARCHION has been reduced to 25% as planned, in line with the ownership structure in ARCHION foreseen as part of the business integration of Mitsubishi Fuso Truck and Bus Corporation (“Mitsubishi Fuso”) and Hino Motors, Ltd. (“Hino”). As part of the secondary offering, Daimler Truck generated cash proceeds of approximately €600 million.
Eva Scherer, CFO of Daimler Truck: “By reducing our shareholding in ARCHION to 25%, we have reached an important strategic milestone for Daimler Truck. The secondary offering generated cash proceeds of approximately €600 million, increased ARCHION’s free float and completed its successful transition to an independently listed company. We remain a committed shareholder and continue to support ARCHION going forward.”
For Daimler Truck, the business integration of Mitsubishi Fuso and Hino marked an important step to capitalize on its strategic pillar to unlock full potential. Daimler Truck is determined to increase long-term efficiency while strengthening the focus on heavy-duty commercial vehicles and realizing scale, where it creates value.

Leinfelden-Echterdingen – In 1896, Gottlieb Daimler invented the truck, paving the way for the development of road freight transport. 130 years later, Mercedes-Benz Trucks is marking this anniversary with a special edition: the company will present the “130 Years Trucks Edition” at IAA Transportation.
Under the guiding principle “Legacy meets Passion,” the special model combines the history of Mercedes-Benz Trucks with current vehicle technologies. The edition is aimed at customers and drivers who have a close connection to the brand and their truck.
Even before its premiere, one thing is certain: the “130 Years Trucks Edition,” limited to 400 vehicles, is based on the Mercedes-Benz Actros with the ProCabin and will be available with both a diesel powertrain and as a battery-electric truck. Around 30 special equipment features incorporate historic colors, materials and design elements and transfer them into a contemporary vehicle concept.
Mercedes-Benz Trucks will reveal more details about the design, equipment features and available vehicle variants at IAA Transportation.

14 유럽 An overview of the global zero-emission medium- and heavy-duty vehicle market
An overview of the global zero-emission medium- and heavy-duty vehicle market
2026-08-19
August 19, 2026 |By: Shiyue Mao, Eamonn Mulholland, Shuang Li, Felipe Rodriguez, and Ray Minjares
Global zero-emission medium- and heavy-duty vehicle (ZE-MHDV) sales grew considerably from 2020 to 2025, reaching over 520,000 units in 2025. China accounted for about 88% of global sales, though its share has begun to decline as sales in other markets accelerate. Excluding China, global ZE-MHDV sales reached approximately 63,000 units in 2025, led by the United States, the European Union (EU), and India. The United States accounted for about 40% of non-China volumes, driven primarily by medium-duty electric trucks.
The composition of ZE sales varied sharply by country in 2025. China’s 457,300 ZE-MHDV sales were dominated by heavy- and medium-duty trucks, with buses contributing just 11% of sales. In the United States, medium-duty trucks accounted for over 90% of ZE-MHDV sales, while buses and heavy-duty trucks each represented about 3–4%. Excluding both China and the United States, the ZE-MHDV market in India and Brazil remained predominantly driven by buses and coaches, which accounted for over half of sales across these regions, while sales of ZE medium- and heavy-duty trucks remained minimal.
The ICCT Global EV Market Monitor: Trucks and Buses covers 34 countries, which represent around 80% of total global truck and bus sales and 99% of zero-emission truck and bus sales. Updated quarterly, it gives policymakers, researchers, and fleet operators a reliable picture of where adoption stands, how markets compare, and where momentum is building.
Sales growth was particularly strong in the ZE medium-duty truck and van segment (3.5–12 tonnes), where the ZE sales share more than doubled from 10.4% in 2024 to 21.0% in 2025. The ZE bus and coach market, still largely driven by ZE city buses, also expanded significantly. The sales share of ZE city buses rose from 45% to 58% between 2024 and 2025, while the sales share of ZE interurban buses and coaches remained stagnant at around 4% across both years.
Brazil’s ZE-MHDV growth has been driven primarily by urban buses, supported by federal fleet-renewal initiatives such as Novo PAC Seleções, dedicated financing, and municipal transport decarbonization policies. Truck electrification remains limited. The Green Mobility and Innovation (MOVER) Program is expected to introduce CO2 emission-reduction targets for new HDVs, but these targets are not expected before 2029.
Performance grading and ultra-low emission campaigns have strengthened clean transportation requirements in key industries, while provincial new energy vehicle procurement targets have driven local ZE-MHDV adoption. Charging and battery-swapping infrastructure and battery-swapping standards have further accelerated adoption.
Buses and coaches accounted for the vast majority (87%) of ZE sales (5,021 units, 4.4% ZE share within the segment), with heavy-duty trucks (239 units, 0.3% ZE share) and medium-duty trucks (528 units, 0.1% ZE share) making up the balance. Sales in 2025 increased 21% year-over-year, and the overall ZE-MHDV market grew from 1,254 units in 2021, a 362% cumulative increase.
India’s PM E-DRIVE scheme subsidizes electric bus and truck purchases. At the state level, electric vehicle policies include purchase incentives and registration fee exemptions. The rising share of ZE buses can also be attributed to demand aggregation schemes like the PM-eBus Sewa and the National Electric Bus Program.
Buses and coaches dominated with 2,932 units, led by manufacturer Wrightbus (1,007 units), which held a 28% share of the United Kingdom’s bus manufacturer market. Heavy-duty trucks followed with 464 units (1.3% ZE share) and medium-duty trucks with 213 units (3.0% ZE share).
The United Kingdom has pledged to end sales of new non-ZE heavy goods vehicles under 26 tonnes by 2035 and all new heavy goods vehicles by 2040, with a formal CO2 regulatory framework for heavy goods vehicles under consultation.
The Phase 3 greenhouse gas standards were recently repealed, and Congress has revoked California and 10 other states’ waiver to implement the Advanced Clean Trucks rule. Federal fiscal incentives for ZE-MHDVs were also significantly reduced. As a result, ZE-MHDV deployment is now driven primarily by fleet economics that take advantage of electric HDVs’ lower total cost of ownership relative to diesel counterparts, helped by state incentive programs.
The ICCT’sGlobal EV Market Monitor: Trucks and Busescovers 34 countries, which represent around 80% of total global truck and bus sales and 99% of zero-emission truck and bus sales.
Join ourmailing listto keep up with ICCT’s latest research and analysis.
 ICCT (International Council on Clean Transportation)

25 유럽 Electric mobility in China: New country dossier from NOW GmbH
Electric mobility in China: New country dossier from NOW GmbH
2026-08-12
Berlin, August 2026.China is continuing to expand its role as the world’s leading market for electric mobility. With around 44 million electric passenger cars in use and more than 20 million charging points, China now has the world’s largest fleet of electric vehicles and the most extensive charging network. At the same time, the country is increasingly developing into a global centre of innovation and production for batteries and electric vehicles. These are the findings of the updated China country dossier from NOW GmbH’s ‘Electric Mobility International’ series.
The country dossier analyses the development of battery-electric mobility and hydrogen mobility in China, describes the policy framework and support mechanisms, and assesses current market trends.
Alongside the successes, the dossier also highlights current challenges. After years of strong growth, the first signs of market consolidation are emerging in the Chinese automotive industry. This is occurring at the same time as Chinese manufacturers’ export activities are expanding.
This publication is aimed at policymakers, public administration, academia, the business community and all stakeholders who follow international developments in the field of electric mobility and hydrogen technologies.
The country dossier “Electric Mobility International: China” is now available to download in German language free of charge:Elektromobilität international: China (PDF)
 NOW GmbH Press Releases
26 유럽 European Union CO2 standards for cars and vans: Low-carbon steel credits
European Union CO2 standards for cars and vans: Low-carbon steel credits
2026-08-06
August 6, 2026 |By:  Marta Negri and Georg Bieker
The European Commission’s proposed amendments to the carbon dioxide (CO2) standards for passenger cars and vans would allow manufacturers to use low-carbon steel credits to permit residual tailpipe CO2emissions of new vehicles after 2035. Because the emissions of steel production are already covered in the policy framework of the Emissions Trading System (ETS), however, the additional tailpipe emissions are not expected to be compensated by additional emission reductions in steel production.
Despite this, if the low-carbon steel credit mechanism is designed to support fossil-free steel investments in the EU, the credits can provide demand certainty for fossil-free production capacity and support the steel sector in meeting its long-term decarbonization requirements under the ETS.
This policy brief finds that there is enough announced capacity in the EU to limit steel credits to fossil-free steel.By contrast, including coal-based steelmaking and not limiting the eligibility of recycled steel would create windfall credits and displacement effects and risk delaying the transition to fossil-free steel. Additionally, a fair translation of steel-related emissions into the CO2standards requires calibrating the Commission’s proposed formula to define the baseline emissions intensity with EU-specific values, only accounting for the steel quantity that ends up in finished vehicles, and applying a real-world correction factor.
Join ourmailing listto keep up with ICCT’s latest research and analysis.
 ICCT (International Council on Clean Transportation)

28 유럽 New course: Bidirectional charging in the LadeLernTOOL
New course: Bidirectional charging in the LadeLernTOOL
2026-07-30
The free e-learning course is particularly geared towards municipal decision-makers as well as planning experts. Energy providers, municipal utilities, grid operators and companies with charging infrastructure or electric fleets will also benefit.
The 30-minute course provides information on the technical fundamentals as well as the relevant economic, regulatory and practical issues. Participants learn how electric vehicles can be used in future as flexible energy storage systems and what role they can play in a stable and sustainable energy system.
Johannes Pallasch, Director and Spokesperson of the National Centre for Charging Infrastructure:“Bidirectional charging offers great potential to make electric mobility even more attractive to consumers. Our course provides the necessary foundational knowledge and demonstrates what is already possible today. At the same time, we are at the early stages of development and we must better understand how people will use the various applications in their daily lives in future.”
In a series of three thematic modules, the course addresses key aspects of bidirectional charging: It explains the differences between controlled charging and bidirectional applications such as Vehicle-to-Home, Vehicle-to-Building, and Vehicle-to-Grid. In addition, technical and organisational prerequisites are outlined – from vehicles and wall boxes to communication standards and smart metering systems. Another focus issue is current market development, specific application examples as well as a realistic evaluation of opportunities and existing challenges. The compact course is designed to take around 30 minutes, in addition to around five minutes for a final test.
Interactive elements such as hotspots, flip cards and practical exercises support the learning process and enable a direct knowledge transfer. At the same time, the course addresses widespread myths surrounding bidirectional charging and sets the record straight. These include the concern that bidirectional charging heavily strains the vehicle battery or that range is lost when electricity is fed back into the grid. In this way, participants receive a well-founded and objective overview of the current status of the technology. Following successful completion,  participants receive a certificate confirming their knowledge about bidirectional charging.
Following a short registration process, the LadeLernTOOL is available for use free of charge. Alongside the StandortTOOL, the FlächenTOOL and the MasterplanTOOL, the LadeLernTOOL belongs to the four central instruments of the TOOLBox that provides custom and data-based support to municipalities expanding their local charging infrastructure. All TOOLs are made available by the National Centre for Charging Infrastructure under the NOW GmbH umbrella.
On behalf of the Federal Ministry of Transport, the National Centre for Charging Infrastructure coordinates and manages activities for the expansion of charging infrastructure in Germany. It supports the planning, implementation and funding of charging infrastructure. In order to better understand the demand for charging stations, it collects relevant data. It connects all major stakeholders and shares its expertise. Their focus is always on the consumer.
Further information can be found here:www.nationale-leitstelle.de
 NOW GmbH Press Releases

1 북미 MAN begins series production of megawatt trucks
MAN begins series production of megawatt trucks
2026-08-19
Germany– At the end of July,MAN Truck & Bus SE– a subsidiary ofTraton– made its first electric truck capable of charging at megawatt standard on the production line in Munich, Bavaria, thus claiming to be the first European manufacturer to produce an MCS-ready truck in series.
The first MAN electric trucks capable of charging at rates of up to 750 kilowatts are now being delivered to customers across Europe. At 750 kW, a truck carrying six batteries with a total capacity of 534 kilowatt-hours can recharge from 20% to 80% in less than thirty minutes; with seven batteries and 623 kWh, the charging time is less than forty minutes. MAN electric trucks that are currently on the road have a maximum charging capacity of just 375 kW using the Combined Charging System standard.

Daimler Trucks uses real-world logistics data to make case for electric long-haul operations
2026-08-19
Germany–Daimler Truck AGis using real-world operational data from its own logistics network and customer fleets to argue that battery-electric trucks are already capable of reliable and economically viable operation. The supporting telemetry study, conducted between November 2025 and March 2026, analysed more than 3,000 tours and 3,100 charging events from more than 15 vehicles. Separately, approximately 80 battery-electric Mercedes-Benz trucks are currently operating within the company's production network, predominantly eActros 600s, which have collectively covered more than six million electric miles.
The results include a 600-kilometre daily route between Wörth am Rhein and Bielefeld operated by logistics company Seifert, where an eActros 600 running at an average 36-tonne gross combination weight reportedly consumed 100 kWh/100 km between November and February. A second application operated by Logistik Schmitt covers 352 kilometres per day over eight shuttle runs, with an average consumption of 114 kWh/100 km, while FERCAM's route from South Tyrol to Wörth covers around 600 kilometres daily at up to 42 tonnes, averaging 92 kWh/100 km.

4 북미 U.S. trailer orders rise 97% in July
U.S. trailer orders rise 97% in July
2026-08-19
U.S. trailer orders defied seasonal expectations in July, though the resulting gain from the prior year was against lukewarm results.
ACT Research preliminary data on Aug. 18 showed orders jumped 97% fromthe previous yearto 15,900 units and also marked an 18%improvement over June.
“Typically, July is the weakest net order month of the annual ordering cycle, as fleets have made their decisions for current-year needs and OEMs start to build down the backlog,” saidJennifer McNealy, director of commercial vehicle market research for ACT. “That said, this year’s cycle has been anything but ordinary.”
McNealy added that the order strength that should have started in September or October of last year didn’t begin until December. She also views the atypical strength in orders as continuing to reflect an improving freight market that has been supported by rising rates.
“Regardless of the timing, the increased order activity is certainly welcome, but premature in terms of 2027 order timing and the opening by OEMs of next year’s calendars,” McNealy said. “Additionally, caution remains a strategy for some trailer purchasers.”
The past few years have been hard for carriers even with rates rising now, she added. McNealy warned that pent-up demand and the risk of higher maintenance costs to repair, rather than purchase new equipment, remain counterweights to their decision-making process.
“In a recent monthly survey, OEMs shared with ACT Research that the uptick in order activity seems to be a threefold event,” McNealy said. “Customers ordering ahead of tariff-related price increases, healthier long-term business conditions and pent-up replacement demand.”

Commercial motor vehicles will face stiffer penalties for certain violations on Laredo, Texas bridges.

On itsFacebook page, Port Laredo said that every time a U-turn occurs, an impact is felt by drivers as well as bridge administrators.
The Laredo Port of Entry Advisory Committee estimates each U-turn adds 5-7 minutes of additional delays for commercial traffic.
On average, nearly 130 U-turns are recorded each month across the bridge system, according to port data.
All violations are also subject to applicable per-axle toll fees.
A notice on theCity of Laredo Bridge Systemwebsite said that any automated vehicle identification account with more than three insufficient-funds U-turn violations will be suspended and assessed a $200 reactivation fee.
All commercial account holders are required to maintain a valid credit card on file and enroll in automatic replenishment, according to this notice.
“We’re not in the business of canceling accounts,” Felipe Romero, director of marketing and communications at Port Laredo, toldKGNS-TV. “We’re in the business of moving traffic, our commercial traffic.”

9 북미 ABO Energy Sells H2 Hub to Tyczka Hydrogen
ABO Energy Sells H2 Hub to Tyczka Hydrogen
2026-08-19
Wiesbaden -- ABO Energy has sold a hydrogen project – comprising an electrolyser, a hydrogen refuelling station, and a trailer filling station – to Tyczka Hydrogen GmbH. The plant is located in Huenfeld, Germany (Hesse), and has been producing RNFBO-certified green hydrogen for fuel cell busses and lorries since August 2025. RNFBO stands for Renewable Fuel of Non-Biological Origin and refers to advanced fuels that reduce greenhouse gas emissions.
“We have successfully realised this complex project, from the planning stage through to construction and commissioning. We are therefore all the more delighted to have found a strong buyer in Tyczka Hydrogen,” says Dr Karsten Schlageter, Managing Director at ABO Energy.
Following several years of planning and 13 months of construction, ABO Energy inaugurated the project and commissioned it in the summer of 2025. In July 2025, the plant became one of the first in Germany to receive TÜV certification under the CertifHy EU RNFBO Voluntary Scheme for green hydrogen. Since then, the 5-megawatt electrolyser has been producing up to 450 tonnes of hydrogen annually for the 350-bar refuelling station and the hydrogen filling station. The electricity required for this is generated by wind and solar farms. ABO Kraft & Wärme AG has been responsible for operational management since the opening.
The project is being funded as part of the National Innovation Programme for Hydrogen and Fuel Cell Technology with a total of twelve million euros by the German Federal Ministry for Digital and Transport Affairs. Funding for this measure is also being provided as part of the German Recovery and Resilience Plan via the European Recovery and Resilience Facilities (ARF) in the NextGenerationEU programme. The funding guideline is coordinated by NOW GmbH and implemented by Project Management Jülich (PtJ).

Aerial view of the Kawasaki Works

For Asahi Kasei, a diversified global company, this represents a significant milestone in accelerating its strategic portfolio transformation.
Asahi Kasei is a comprehensive manufacturer and supplier of alkaline water electrolyzers for hydrogen production. The company is building new facilities for both cell frames and membranes for electrolysis at its plant site in Kawasaki, Kanagawa Prefecture, Japan, targeting a manufacturing capacity of at least 2 GW each by 2028. Together with its current capacity for ion-exchange membrane process chlor-alkali electrolysis equipment, this expansion will raise the company’s total annual capacity for cell frames and membranes to more than 3 GW.

This will help strengthen the global clean hydrogen supply base while raising Japan's industrial competitiveness in the field of hydrogen.
Configuration of Asahi Kasei's alkaline water electrolyzer

Backed by the various support initiatives, the company will further expand capacity while contributing to regional economic development.
Looking ahead, Asahi Kasei has stated it will strengthen its technology base and supply capabilities in water electrolysis, while leveraging the complementary strengths of its hydrogen and chlor-alkali operations to build on its global competitiveness.
Asahi Kasei is a diversified global company that contributes to life and living for people around the world. Since its foundation in 1922, with businesses in ammonia and cellulose fiber, Asahi Kasei has consistently grown through proactive portfolio transformation to meet the evolving needs of every age. With 50,000 employees worldwide, the company contributes to sustainability by providing solutions to the world’s challenges across its three business sectors: Healthcare, Homes, and Material. Small projects lift Home Depot quarterly sales
2026-08-18
Home Depot’s sales improved during its second quarter as customers focused on smaller projects during the summer months with the U.S. housing sector still mired in a slump.
Revenue increased to $47.86 billion from $45.28 billion, edging out the $47.24 billion that Wall Street had expected, according to a survey by FactSet.
Globally, sales at stores open at least a year, a key indicator of a retailer’s health, climbed 1.7%. In the U.S., comparable store sales rose 1.3%.
“We saw broad-based demand across the business as customers continued to engage in smaller projects,” Chief Financial Officer Richard McPhail said in prepared remarks Aug. 18.
Customer transactions slipped 1% in the quarter, but the amount shoppers spent rose to $92.50 per average receipt from $90.01 a year earlier.
Healthy spending on smaller projects during the quarter is encouraging, said Neil Saunders, managing director of GlobalData.
“From our data, the number of smaller projects undertaken during the quarter increased by 1.5% over the prior year,” Saunders said in an email. “This may sound unimpressive, but it represents a step change from the declines of previous periods."
But Saunders also pointed out a potential weakness that is impacting almost all businesses where credit is sometimes required.
“The number of bigger-ticket projects undertaken remains down, falling by 2.1% over last year,” he said. "Concerns around financing and a previous lack of moving activity both remain major drags on the bigger-ticket segment.”
Homeowners that are currently considering taking out loans using home equity are realizing that it is considerably more expensive now compared with the ultra-low rates Americans could count on in the early 2020s. A home project that seemed within reach at a 4% to 5% borrowing rate looks a lot more daunting at 8% or higher.
Existing-home sales decreased by 1.7% in July. Year-over-year sales rose in the Midwest and West, and were flat in the Northeast and South. Learn more:https://t.co/zeDXgcl2nWpic.twitter.com/BOtjCstWSd— National Association of REALTORS® (@nardotrealtor)August 17, 2026

The U.S. housing market has been in a slump dating to 2022, the year mortgage rates began climbing from historic lows that fueled a homebuying frenzy at the start of this decade.
Sales of previously occupied U.S. homes slowed again in July as record prices and the highest mortgage rates in a year prove to be an insurmountable hurdle for many prospective buyers. Existing home sales fell 1.7% last month from June, the National Association of Realtors said last week.
Home prices continue to rise and hit unprecedented levels for the month of July, NAR said. The U.S. median sales price increased 2% from a year earlier, to $434,100.
For the three months ended Aug. 2, Home Depot earned $4.77 billion, or $4.79 per share. A year earlier the home improvement retailer earned $4.55 billion, or $4.58 per share.
Excluding one-time items, earnings were $4.92 per share. That’s much better than the $4.73 per share that Wall Street predicted.
Brad Gulick of Eaton Mobile Power Group discusses hydraulic systems that power trucks. He addresses dump pump sizing and more. Tune in above or by going toRoadSigns.ttnews.com.
Home Depot also announced Aug. 18 that it is launching express delivery nationwide. The service will get orders to customers within three hours or less. It will be available for a small flat fee in the U.S., with no subscription or membership required.
The Atlanta company has posted solid back-to-back quarterly performances this year though many Americans have struggled to buy a home. Last year, the company said that it didn't expect to raise prices because of tariffs, but executive Billy Bastek said at the time that some products that were on Home Depot shelves may disappear.

14 북미 Recall Roundup: NHTSA targets truck wipers, marker lights, turn signals, and liftgates
Recall Roundup: NHTSA targets truck wipers, marker lights, turn signals, and liftgates
2026-08-18
The National Highway Traffic Safety Administration(NHTSA) recently issued recall notices for commercial vehicles, including brands from Rivian, Mack, and more.
Owners of the affected vehicles should be contacted via U.S. mail over the next two months, but fleet and independent owners of these vehicles can contact the NHTSA Safety Hotline at 888-327-4236 or visitnhtsa.govfor more details. More information on each recall can be found below.
International Motorsis recalling certain 2027 HV, HX, LT, MV, and RH trucks. A retaining nut in the wiper assembly may loosen, causing both windshield wipers to disconnect from the motor. Inoperative windshield wipers can reduce the driver's visibility, increasing the risk of a crash.
This recall potentially affects 49 vehicles. Dealers will tighten the wiper motor drive nut and three motor mounting bolts. Owner notification letters are expected to be mailed October 5. Owners may contact International customer service at 331-332-5000. International's number for this recall is26513.
US Truck Bodyis recalling certain 2025 Isuzu NPR HD vehicles equipped with Waltco MDV series liftgates. The deck pins may break, causing the liftgate platform to fall. A liftgate that falls unexpectedly increases the risk of injury.
This recall potentially affects 31 vehicles. US Truck Body will work with the supplier,Hiab, to inspect the platform bushings and hydraulic systems and replace the deck pins. All affected vehicles are expected to be repaired by August 27. Owners maycontactUS Truck Body customer service at 800-443-0843.
Mack Trucksis recalling certain 2027 Anthem AN (4) and Pioneer PR (4) vehicles. The center roof marker lights may fail to illuminate. As such, these vehicles fail to comply with the requirements of Federal Motor Vehicle Safety Standard number 108, "Lamps, Reflective Devices, and Associated Equipment." Marker lights that fail to illuminate can reduce visibility, increasing the risk of a crash.
This recall potentially affects 513 vehicles. Dealers will replace the center roof marker light. Owner notification letters are expected to be mailed October 5. Owners may contact Mack's customer service at 800-866-1177. Mack's number for this recall isSC0498.
Rivian Automotiveis recalling certain 2025-2026 R1S and R1T vehicles. One or both front turn signals may fail to illuminate and flash. As such, these vehicles fail to comply with the requirements of Federal Motor Vehicle Safety Standard (FMVSS) number 108, "Lamps, Reflective Devices, and Associated Equipment." Turn signals that do not function properly increase the risk of a crash.
This recall potentially affects 3,829 vehicles. Rivian will inspect and replace the front turn signals as necessary. Owner notification letters are expected to be mailed September 29. Owners may contact Rivian customer service at 888-748-4261. Rivian's number for this recall isFSAM-1833. This is an expansion of recall 25V387.
Volvo Trucks North America (VTNA)is recalling certain 2027 VNL (4) and VNR (4) vehicles. The center roof marker lights may fail to illuminate. As such, these vehicles fail to comply with the requirements of Federal Motor Vehicle Safety Standard number 108, "Lamps, Reflective Devices, and Associated Equipment." Marker lights that fail to illuminate can reduce visibility, increasing the risk of a crash.
This recall potentially affects 1,851 vehicles. Dealers will replace the center roof marker light. Owner notification letters are expected to be mailed October 5. Owners may contact Volvo Trucks' customer service at 800-528-6586. Volvo Trucks' number for this recall isRVXX2608.

15 북미 Trucks nailed for left lane use
Trucks nailed for left lane use
2026-08-18
Commercial vehicles are prohibited from driving in the left lane on the Atchafalaya Basin Bridge in Louisiana.
The use of enforcement cameras to track vehicle times on the 18-mile bridge on Interstate 10 was authorized in 2022. In 2024, thatlaw was repealed, and double fines for speeding on the bridge were eliminated.
During a recent enforcement operation, the Louisiana State Police said the agency issued 26 citations to trucks that were driving in the left lane of the bridge in St. Martin and Iberville parishes.
On itsFacebook page, state police said it has received numerous complaints about commercial vehicles in the left lane.
“Commercial motor vehicles traveling in the left lane are violating the law and can create serious safety and traffic concerns,” the Louisiana State Police said.
Local law enforcement said the citations were issued for disregarding traffic control devices requiring commercial motor vehicles to remain in the right lane. Four other safety violations were identified, and one driver was placed out of service.
State police added that it plans to continue proactive enforcement efforts to improve safety and keep traffic moving along the Atchafalaya Basin Bridge.
A recent evaluation of the statewide commercial vehicle size and weight enforcement programscalled into questionpractices employed by the Louisiana Department of Transportation and Development and Louisiana State Police.
“Trucks that are potentially overweight and oversize but lack required permits are driving on state highways and bridges, illegally bypassing weigh stations and contributing to the deterioration of road conditions and the overall driving environment,” the Louisiana Legislative Auditor said.
The audit noted that state law does not clearly define each agency’s area of jurisdiction and would need to be amended to allow for the use of technologies, such as virtual weigh stations and license plate recognition.
State law also limits the ability to collect civil penalties for size and weight violations from out-of-state trucking companies.
In 2025, the American Society of Civil Engineers graded roads in Louisiana a “D,” while bridges were a “D+.”LL

20 북미 Trucker’s fuel fraud scheme costs company over half a million dollars
Trucker’s fuel fraud scheme costs company over half a million dollars
2026-08-12
A trucker in West Virginia has pleaded guilty to a fuel fraud scheme that cost his employer over half a million dollars.
On Thursday, July 30, the U.S. Attorney’s Office, Southern District of West Virginia, announced that Jeffrey Jeffers, 35, pleaded guilty to one count of wire fraud. According to court documents, Jeffers admitted to stealing $510,465.19 from his company through false fuel charges to company-issued credit cards.
Once the transactions were complete, Jeffers would then transfer the funds into an account he owned. Prosecutors said the address listed for the companies was the address of one of Jeffers’ relatives in Canton, Ohio.
Court records show that Jeffers’ truck was fueled at the carrier’s distribution facility in Wood County prior to leaving for his route, but the company provided fuel cards in case of emergency refueling. Despite racking up over half a million in charges, in his plea, Jeffers admitted that his delivery routes typically never required refueling outside of the distribution facility.
Jeffers is scheduled to be sentenced on Nov. 9. He faces a maximum sentence of 20 years in prison, up to three years of supervised release, and a fine of up to $250,000. Jeffers is also ordered to pay $510,465.19 in restitution.LL

22 북미 EEOC claims trucking school discriminated against females
EEOC claims trucking school discriminated against females
2026-08-10
Female student drivers allege they were subjected to unlawful policies and practices, according to a discrimination lawsuit filed against a trucking school operating in Texas and Mississippi.
In this claim, the Equal Employment Opportunity Commission said the “overtly discriminatory training conditions” at KLLM Driving Academy Inc., a subsidiary of KLLM Transport Services, date back to at least January 2021.
The EEOC said that among these conditions was the trucking school’s decision to adopt the male trainers’ decision to opt out of training female student drivers instead of providing separate sleeping quarters during training.
This delayed the start of training for female students, the EEOC claimed in the lawsuit.
“Rather than provide the female student drivers’ single-sex sleeping quarters that protected their privacy and ensured equal employment opportunity, the employer here instead decided to adopt a series of sex-based discriminatory policies that treated women trainees worse than male trainees and disadvantaged them in their employment opportunities, which is unlawful disparate treatment,” acting EEOC General Counsel Catherine Eschbach said.
KLLM Driving Academy also mandated that female student drivers willing to receive instruction from male trainers be required to notify their sponsors or partners before training could begin, according to the lawsuit.
“Under federal law, all workers in the United States have a right to be given a fair opportunity to succeed based on their knowledge, skills and effort, not their sex,” EEOC Dallas District Office Director Travis Nicholson said. “That fundamental principle applies to all aspects of their jobs, including training and other terms and conditions of employment.”
The EEOC said the trucking school’s conduct violates the Civil Rights Act of 1964, prohibiting sex discrimination in employment.
Following an attempt to reach a pre-litigation settlement, the EEOC filed the lawsuit in the U.S. District Court for the Northern District of Texas, Dallas Division.
According to that lawsuit, Central Transport, based in Warren, Mich., routinely turned down qualified women truck drivers at terminals across the nation.
Allegations at this trucking company go back at least a decade.
Central Transport must pay $5.5 million in backpay, lost benefits, interest and compensatory damages, according to the settlement.LL

23 북미 How does California’s new $3,500 EV rebate work?
How does California’s new $3,500 EV rebate work?
2026-08-10
First-time electric vehicle buyers in California could soon get thousands of dollars off the sticker price. Here’s how to apply.
Thinking about buying or leasing anelectric carin the near future? California will soon be making that cheaper.
On Friday, Gov. Gavin Newsom launched “MyFirstEV,” a new state program that sets aside millions of dollars to fund rebates for residents who buy or lease a zero-emission vehicle — a category that includes battery-electric cars andhydrogen fuel cell-powered vehicles.
First-time EV buyers can qualify for a $3,500 discount when buying or leasing a new electric vehicle, as long as the retail price is under $50,000. If you’re looking for ausedelectric car, there’s still a price reduction available — a smaller one, however: $1,750 off for vehicles retailing for under $25,000.
The state’s program comes a year after President Donald Trump’s massive spending and tax plan known as the One Big Beautiful Billendedfederal tax credits for EVs nationwide. Previously, American consumers could claim a $7,500 tax credit after buying a new EV or $4,000 for used EVs.
When announcing the program in July, Newsom said that as the federal government pulls back from supporting EVs, California would instead be “putting its foot on the accelerator” — and that the instant rebate program would “[make] it easier for families to drive clean, breathe clean and keep more money in their pockets.”
The program has secured $270 million in funding — half of that from the state budget and the other from participating EV automakers.
Only California residents who are buying or leasing an EV for the first time are eligible for this rebate.
And consumers will have to confirm that this is the first time they are buying or leasing an EV before taking their car home, said Lindsay Buckley, communications director of the California Air Resources Board, the agency tasked with managing the program.
“Participants will be required to sign a legal document declaring that this is in fact their first purchase or lease of an electric vehicle,” she said.
“So if you’ve already bought or leased an electric vehicle in the past, then you wouldn’t be eligible for this program.”
Limiting the program to first-time buyers could actually help boost the popularity of EVs among people who have never bought them, said Scott Moura, a UC Berkeley professor of civil engineering.
“Providing incentive to people who have bought EVs before isn’t really adding to the number of people who purchase EVs,” he said. “The funds can be used most effectively if they’re targeted towards first-time EV buyers.”
No — there’s no application to fill out ahead of time. All you need to do is go to a dealership of one of the15 participating automakers.
Hyundai, Lucid and Tesla rebates are available starting today. The other participants will launch their programs throughout the fall.
This is different from other past state rebate programs — like the now-terminatedElectric Bicycle Incentive Program— which have required participants to fill out an applicationbeforemaking a purchase.
If you move forward with making a purchase or lease, confirm two things with the salesperson and the financing team:
When federal EV rebates were available, buyers had to initially wait until they filed their taxes the year after buying their car to request this money back. But state officials say that folks interested in the FirstEV discount won’t have to wait so long.
“Californians will be able to go down to participating automakers’ dealerships and access the rebates at the point of sale,” Buckley said. “They won’t have any delay in getting this discount.”
No — MyFirstEV discounts will only cover battery-electric cars and hydrogen fuel cell-powered vehicles from automakers participating in the program.
Hybrid vehicles are also not included in MyFirstEV, state officials confirmed with KQED.
There’s also a price limit: The EV you choose must cost under $50,000 if it’s a new car, and $25,000 if it’s used.
There is, however, asmall exceptionto this price rule if the automaker is headquartered in California — in which case the discounts will apply regardless of the manufacturer’s retail price.
More than adozen electric car brandsare based in the Golden State, with several selling models priced beyond the $50,000 limit.
While the MyFirstEV discount will not vary based on income, consumers can stack the discount with other incentive programs, including theDriving Clean Assistance ProgramandClean Cars 4 All.
To qualify for Clean Cars 4 All, residents must have an income of less than or equal to 300% of the federal poverty level. This program could unlock up to $12,000 in additional savings.
No — funds will be divided equally among the participating automakers.
However, there may be greater demand for some brands, which could mean that rebates may run out faster at some dealerships.

24 북미 Hydrogen: New Bosch fuel cell system begins test operations in Madrid
Hydrogen: New Bosch fuel cell system begins test operations in Madrid
2026-08-09
Hydrogen: New Bosch fuel cell system begins test operations in Madrid
Bosch’s new fuel-cell system is being used in an Irizar test bus. Bus operator Alsa plans to conduct a trial lasting several weeks in Madrid’s public transit system. Emissions-free powertrain enables long ranges of over 1,000 kilometers with short refueling times.
Stuttgart, Germany and Madrid, Spain – In Europe, pressure on urban public transit is mounting: in light of mandatory EU requirements to reduce carbon emissions from city buses by 90 percent by 2030, public transit operators are seeking reliable, emissions-free solutions. To this end, an initial test operation is now launching in Madrid: Equipped with a fuel-cell power module (FCPM) from Bosch, an Irizar bus has been transporting passengers through the Spanish capital for several weeks now. Vehicle operation will focus on routes where the fuel cell’s long range allows it to fully demonstrate its advantages. The operator is the Spanish bus company Alsa.
Over the past few weeks, the test vehicle has already undergone extensive testing and received the necessary approval. Day-to-day operation in real conditions will now confirm the robustness and strengths of the fuel-cell powertrain. The test bus is equipped with the FCPM C190 fuel-cell system from Bosch. This compact system is based on a horizontal double stack and delivers a continuous output of 190 kilowatts. With the available hydrogen tank capacity, ranges of over 1,000 kilometers are possible, with refueling times of just 10 to 15 minutes. This means the bus can also be used for intercity service. In addition to the FCPM C190, Bosch also offers the FCPM C300, a system designed for heavy-duty tour buses and trucks. The portfolio is rounded off by the FCPM C100, which, with a continuous power output of 100 kilowatts and a flat design, is the ideal solution for city buses.
Bosch is strongly committed to building an H2economy, and is developing technical solutions for the production, infrastructure, and use of hydrogen. In 2025, the company announced the launch of its Hybrion PEM electrolysis stack for hydrogen production. Bosch is also working on technology for hydrogen engines and offers corresponding components for port and direct injection. At the end of 2025, a team of developers from Bosch won the German Future Prize, the federal president’s award for technology and innovation, for their development of the mobile fuel cell.

25 북미 LG Chem Doubles Lifespan of Key Electrode for Green Hydrogen
LG Chem Doubles Lifespan of Key Electrode for Green Hydrogen
2026-08-09
LG Chem Doubles Lifespan of Key Electrode for Green Hydrogen
PEM Water Electrolysis Electrode Gains Performance and DurabilityResults Published in Nature Communications
LG Chem (051910.KS) has developed a polymer electrolyte membrane (PEM) water electrolysis electrode technology that enables stable, long-term hydrogen production using only a small amount of iridium, raising the prospects for commercializing next-generation green hydrogen production materials.
LG Chem said on the 26th that a research team at its Basic Technology Research Institute, under the Chief Technology Officer (CTO), has developed an interface stabilization technology that dramatically improves the performance and durability of PEM water electrolysis electrodes.
The research, which involved a team led by Ko Jae-hyun, a senior researcher at the Korea Institute of Science and Technology (KIST), was published on the 21st in Nature Communications, one of the world’s top-tier academic journals.
Green hydrogen is a key energy source for realizing a decarbonized society, produced by splitting water using electricity generated from renewable energy. PEM water electrolysis is regarded as a next-generation green hydrogen production technology, based on its high hydrogen productivity and superior output responsiveness.
However, PEM water electrolysis devices use expensive rare-metal iridium catalysts, making economic viability a major challenge. In particular, when less iridium is used in the electrode, performance deteriorates during long-term operation. Shorter equipment replacement cycles and rising operating costs have been the biggest obstacles to expanding the commercialization of PEM water electrolysis technology.
LG Chem solved these problems with a catalyst interface stabilization technology that applies an atomic-level coating layer to the surface of the iridium catalyst. As a result, it succeeded in extending stable hydrogen production time by more than twofold compared with before, while reducing iridium usage by more than half from previous levels.
In addition, LG Chem completed the manufacturing and performance verification of large electrodes based on its own electrode fabrication technology, confirming the potential for mass production and commercialization.
Shim Kyu-seok,Executive Vice President and CTO of LG Chem, said:
This research achievement is a case in which LG Chem’s materials technology competitiveness has been recognized worldwide,
“We will continue to expand various research and development efforts to secure competitiveness in next-generation hydrogen production technology.”

26 북미 ACCIONA advances decarbonization at the Port of Valencia with hydrogen technology
ACCIONA advances decarbonization at the Port of Valencia with hydrogen technology
2026-08-09
ACCIONA advances decarbonization at the Port of Valencia with hydrogen technology
ACCIONA is testing a high-capacity hydrogen-powered electric generator at the North Terminal works at the Port of Valencia to reduce emissions and replace fossil fuels in construction machinery. The initiative, already operational on site, will continue in the validation phase until mid-June.
The equipment, developed by French company INOCEL, is based on a 200 kW hydrogen fuel cell and is being used to supply power to a tower crane involved in the KUGIRA caisson dock operations, which are focused on manufacturing the project’s concrete caissons.
In this type of environment, where access to the power grid is not always available, the usual solution would have been to use a diesel generator. However, using diesel would involve high fuel consumption and the emission of around 80 metric tons of CO₂ equivalent during the test period.
What makes this initiative particularly innovative is the equipment’s power capacity, which makes it possible to test hydrogen technology in construction machinery with high energy requirements. This solution opens the door to new alternatives for reducing the use of conventional fuels, especially at sites without a direct connection to the power grid.
The initiative marks progress in the deployment of zero-emission technologies in real-world construction environments and contributes to the decarbonization of ACCIONA’s operations. It is part of the company’s efforts to incorporate innovative energy solutions that help reduce the use of conventional fuels on construction sites and further advance that transition.

28 북미 No more restroom runarounds for New Hampshire truckers
No more restroom runarounds for New Hampshire truckers
2026-08-06
Most workers can use a restroom without asking. Too many truck drivers still have to ask. Some are told “no.”
Truckers keep shelves stocked. They keep freight moving. Yet some are denied a restroom while picking up or delivering a load.
Washington was the first state to take on the problem. A2023 lawrequires shippers, receivers and terminal operators to provide restroom access.
Now, afederal billwould bring those protections to truck drivers across the country.
TheOwner-Operator Independent Drivers Associationhas supported what it calls a commonsense solution for years.
Truck drivers should not have to search for a restroom while doing the job that keeps America moving.
Truckers should never have to ‘hold it’ because they aren’t allowed to use the restroom. It’s a matter of basic dignity. 🚛🚻 For months, OOIDA worked with@RepHoulahanand@RepTroyNehlsto get restroom access into the new highway bill. THANK YOU for listening to our members!…pic.twitter.com/KTXZDt1MgR
— Owner-Operator Independent Drivers Association (@OOIDA)May 21, 2026
Starting Tuesday, Aug. 11, businesses in New Hampshire can no longer turn truck drivers away from restrooms.
Gov. Kelly Ayotte signed a law requiring businesses to allow truckers to use existing restrooms during deliveries and pickups.
Supporters said too many drivers are denied access. Others simply do not want truck drivers using their facilities.
Rep. Henry Giasson, R-Goffstown, said restroom access is one of the biggest concerns he hears from female truck drivers.
Truckers help keep businesses running. Giasson said allowing them to use a restroom is the least businesses can do.
The lawapplies to most public-facing businesses. It also covers shippers, receivers, factories, warehouses, distribution centers and other loading sites.
Businesses will not have to build new restrooms. They only have to allow truck drivers to use restrooms already available to workers or customers.
Rep. Greg Hill, R-Merrimack, said it is hard to believe lawmakers had to step in.
“I just can’t believe in this day and age that someone is refusing restrooms to another person. I think it’s important that we send a strong signal with this one,” Hill said.
The law includes limits to protect businesses.
Access can be denied if allowing a driver inside would create a health, safety or security risk.
Fuel stations and restaurants with employee-only restrooms are still exempt.
It’s crazy that restroom access keeps popping up, but here we are.
New Hampshire just passed a law giving truck drivers a basic human right they are too often denied.👉https://t.co/tFWrgDOQSZpic.twitter.com/RXCXBztTu0
— Land Line Magazine (@Land_Line_Mag)June 23, 2026
The New Hampshire Motor Transport Association supported the bill.
Bob Sculley is the group’s president. He called restroom access a matter of basic respect.
If a business provides a restroom for employees or customers, he said truck drivers making deliveries should be able to use it too.
OOIDA said the law addresses a problem drivers face every day.
Executive Vice President Lewie Pugh said using a restroom should not turn into a fight.
“But way too often, professional drivers are told no when they ask to use a restroom while picking up or delivering cargo,” Pugh said.
The new law also protects drayage truck drivers working at ports and rail yards.
Drivers must have access to safe restrooms or portable toilets where they are allowed to work.
Terminal operators must provide enough facilities to meet demand.
If long restroom lines become a problem, more facilities must be added.
Operators must also provide parking so drivers have a safe place to stop while using the restroom.LL

30 북미 MAN begins road testing in Bayernflotte hydrogen project
MAN begins road testing in Bayernflotte hydrogen project
2026-08-03
The trial shows MAN treating hydrogen as a niche complement to battery-electric trucks rather than a mainstream alternative
MAN Truck & Bus has begun road testing hydrogen-powered trucks under its Bayernflotte hydrogen project, the final phase of a research programme funded by the Bavarian State Ministry of Economic Affairs, Regional Development and Energy. Three fuel cell trucks have been handed to LoadFox Transport Solutions GmbH for regular logistics operations over three months.
The trucks will run on routes between MAN’s Munich, Dachau and Nuremberg sites, plus additional routes across southern Germany. LoadFox, an in-house transport firm set up by MAN, also tests prototype and autonomous vehicles and digital services under real-world conditions.
The trucks convert gaseous hydrogen into electrical energy via a fuel cell system from Robert Bosch GmbH, complemented by batteries and a ZF drive axle. Hydrogen is stored in 700-bar tanks from Forvia with a 36-kilogram capacity, giving a range of roughly 450 to 500 kilometres and 350 kW of drive output.
With an unladen weight of about 11.5 tonnes and a gross combination weight of up to 42 tonnes, the trucks are designed for heavy distribution and long-haul transport. Data from the trial will feed into hydrogen technology development across the Traton Group.
MAN said it expects battery-electric vehicles to become the dominant road freight technology, citing energy efficiency, operating costs and emissions reduction. It views hydrogen, including its existing hTGX trucks, as complementary for very long routes or areas with limited charging.

31 북미 How Hyundai Motor Has Reimagined the FIFA World Cup™ Experience Through Mobility, Technology and Innovation
How Hyundai Motor Has Reimagined the FIFA World Cup™ Experience Through Mobility, Technology and Innovation
2026-07-30
From global fan engagement campaigns and record-breaking mobility fleets to breakthrough robotics demonstrations, Hyundai Motor's 27-year partnership with FIFA has evolved far beyond traditional sponsorship, transforming the FIFA World Cup™ experience through mobility, innovation and technology.
While the world's top footballers battled for glory at theFIFA World Cup 2026™, it was a humanoid robot that pulled off the tournament’s most unexpected viral moment. During halftime of a Round of 16 match at New York/New Jersey Stadium,Boston Dynamics' humanoid robotAtlas®emerged from the player tunnel, performed a series of iconic goal celebrations and delivered the ceremonial match ball to the referee.
The activation quickly became one of Hyundai Motor's most recognizable FIFA World Cup™ moments. Behind the scenes,The Training Ground— a documentary-style film showcasing Atlas' preparation — generated more than 16 million views on YouTube.Spot®also featured during the tournament, autonomously monitoring key facilities including the International Broadcast Center in Dallas, highlighting Hyundai Motor Group's growing robotics capabilities and vision for future mobility.
Together, these moments reflected the evolution of Hyundai Motor's nearly three-decade partnership with FIFA—from an automotive sponsorship into a global platform for mobility, innovation and fan engagement.
Spot® patrols key operational sites during the FIFA World Cup 2026™, showcasing the robotics capabilities of Hyundai Motor Group and Boston Dynamics.
A 27-Year Partnership on Football's Biggest Stage
Since 1999, Hyundai Motor has been a FIFA Partner, the highest tier within FIFA's sponsorship structure. The 27-year relationship remains the longest sponsorship tenure in the automotive sector and ranks among FIFA's longest-running commercial partnerships. The company secured the rights in 1999 following the introduction of FIFA's automotive sponsorship category, with an agreement covering 13 FIFA competitions and providing a platform for global growth and brand recognition.
Hyundai Motor made its debut as an official FIFA partner at the 1999 FIFA Women's World Cup in the United States. As FIFA tournaments expanded in scale and reach, so did the company's visibility. By the 2006 FIFA World Cup Germany™, the Hyundai Motor brand was exposed globally for an average of 15 minutes per match across all 64 matches, demonstrating the marketing power of football's biggest stage and helping establish Hyundai Motor among the world's leading automotive brands.
Officials pose for a commemorative photo at the announcement ceremony of Hyundai Motor Company's official partnership with the 2002 FIFA World Cup™ in 1999, including Honorary Chairman Mong-Koo Chung (third from right). The Fleet Behind Football's Biggest Stage

34 북미 OMV receives EUR 450 mn loan from the European Investment Bank for its 140 MW green hydrogen production facility
OMV receives EUR 450 mn loan from the European Investment Bank for its 140 MW green hydrogen production facility
2026-07-22
OMV receives EUR 450 mn loan from the European Investment Bank for its 140 MW green hydrogen production facility
Vienna, July 13, 2026 – The European Investment Bank (“EIB”) has granted a EUR 450 million loan to support the construction of OMV’s green hydrogen production plant, which is set to significantly contribute to the decarbonization of the Schwechat refinery in Lower Austria.
With a total investment of EUR 600 million, this 140 MW plant in Bruck an der Leitha is currently under construction and scheduled to commence operations by the end of 2027. Using renewable energy, OMV will produce up to 23,000 tons of green hydrogen per year.
The new facility will enable the local production of green hydrogen and significantly enhance the sustainability of fuel and chemical products at OMV’s Schwechat refinery. OMV estimates an annual reduction of approximately 150,000 tons of carbon emissions*, corresponding to approximately 10 percent of the Schwechat refinery’s current direct emissions.
Reinhard Florey,Deputy Chairman of the Executive Board and Chief Financial Officer of OMV, said:
The European Investment Bank’s loan commitment is a strong signal of confidence in OMV’s transformation strategy and our ability to deliver large-scale projects that drive industrial decarbonization,
“Producing green hydrogen in Austria enables us to increase the captive use of more sustainable energy in our production and for our innovative products. The construction of one of Europe’s largest green hydrogen plants marks a major milestone in the decarbonization of our industrial processes and underscores our ambition to actively shape the energy transition in Austria and beyond.”
EIB Vice-PresidentKarl Nehammer,during the site visit, said:
Green hydrogen is a cornerstone of Europe’s transition to a competitive, climate-neutral and secure energy system,
“By supporting green hydrogen production at industrial scale, we are strengthening Europe’s competitiveness, reinforcing our energy security and helping decarbonise refining by replacing fossil-based hydrogen with renewable hydrogen. This investment also lays the foundation for the future production of sustainable aviation fuels, supporting the EU’s climate and energy security objectives.”
Under OMV Strategy 2030, the company focuses on capturing market growth for renewable fuels and sustainable chemical feedstock and aims at a production capacity of approximately 900 kilotons by 2030. Key assets already in operation include a co‑processing plant and a 10 MW green hydrogen facility at the Schwechat refinery in Austria. Projects currently under execution include a Sustainable Aviation Fuel/Hydrotreated Vegetable Oil plant at the OMV Petrom Petrobrazi refinery, as well as additional 20 MW and 35 MW green hydrogen projects in Romania, operated by OMV Petrom, and the 140 MW green hydrogen plant under construction in Austria. Together, these investments support OMV’s ambition to achieve net‑zero emissions by 2050.

2026-07-17
Deadly floodingalong waterways in Texas Hill Country has surpassed historic levels reached justone year ago.
TheNational Weather Service Austin/San Antoniohas issued watches, warnings and emergencies throughout the region due to the potential for life-threatening flooding.
A video of a California truck driver caught in the Texas flood showed just how serious this situation is.
Gabriel Pablo drove from Fresno, Calif., to Texas to pick up a load, according tolocal mediareports. In Uvalde County, the flood quickly engulfed Pablo’s truck and swept it away while he was still in the cab.
“At that point, I said, ‘You know what? I better do something,” Pablo told a San Antonio television station. “The truck started slipping, going fast.”
As water rushed into the cab, Pablo managed to open the passenger-side window and climb onto the roof.
The situation remained dicey as the truck continued to be carried by the flood, with Pablo atop the cab recording the ordeal.
Fortunately, the truck eventually got stuck between two trees, preventing the flood from carrying it any farther downstream and allowing rescue crews to reach Pablo.
“I’m just amazed at how things can change in one second,” Pablo said.
According to Texas officials, there has been at least one fatality related to this flood and nearly 30 rescue attempts have been made as of Thursday, July 16.
Evacuations have been ordered for Kerr County andCamp La Jitawas evacuated ahead of the flash flood risk.
“We are dealing with and responding to a flood that is likely going to break records,” Gov. Greg Abbott said in a statement. “There are over 1,300 state personnel from more than 30 agencies that are already activated. More than 800 vehicles, more than 75 boats and 20 aircraft have been deployed. Our primary focus right now and throughout the remainder of this torrential rain is saving lives.”LL

36 북미 Weichai certifies world’s first China VI hydrogen engine
Weichai certifies world’s first China VI hydrogen engine
2026-07-10
With 90% component overlap with diesel, the WP15 lowers the infrastructure and fleet adoption barriers for hydrogen trucking
Weichai Power has certified the world’s first heavy-duty hydrogen internal combustion engine to China’s China VI emission standard, following testing at a state-certified automotive test centre in Tianjin on 3 July 2026. The WP15 was evaluated across a full operating cycle including cold start, high-speed full-load and transient variable load conditions.
The WP15 adopts a hydrogen direct-injection, spark-ignited architecture with a 14.6-litre displacement, producing 447 kW (600 horsepower) and 2,800 N•m of torque. Maximum brake thermal efficiency reaches 46.8%. Weichai says the combination delivers strong power output and low fuel consumption while eliminating direct carbon emissions across the engine’s full operating range.
More than 90% of components are shared with conventional diesel platforms, reducing production, retrofit and maintenance costs. The engine tolerates lower hydrogen purity grades than competing designs, which Weichai says will lower end-user fuel costs and ease the commercial transition from fossil fuels in heavy transport.
Target applications include long-haul trucks, mining dump trucks, port equipment, steel mill vehicles and large hydrogen power generators. The architecture is designed to replicate the load capacity and range of diesel equivalents in sectors where battery-electric solutions face weight and range constraints.
Weichai Power, listed in Hong Kong, has invested in hydrogen powertrain development for over two decades and operates an integrated system covering fundamental research, product engineering, testing and commercial deployment. The firm says mass production will be accelerated following the certification, with active support for China’s national hydrogen demonstration programmes forming a stated near-term priority.
 Automotive World
