Blindaje en la México-Orizaba reduce 70.9% el robo a transporte de carga: Gobierno de Puebla
2026-08-19
El Gobierno de Puebla informó queel operativo desplegado en la autopista México-Orizaba 150 D, en el tramo Amozoc-Esperanza, para combatir el robo a transporte de carga, permitió reducir 70.9% la incidencia de este delito entre enero y juliode este año, en comparación con el mismo periodo de 2025.
En conferencia de prensa, el secretario de Seguridad Pública, Francisco Sánchez González, detalló que durante los primeros siete meses de 2026 se reportaron 184 casos al número de emergencias 911, frente a los 632 registrados en el mismo periodo del año pasado.
Te recomendamos leer:Detienen a líder de banda dedicada al robo de carga en la Puebla-Veracruz
El funcionario señaló que la estrategia implementada en este tramo carretero fue reforzada con 30 puntos de monitoreo inteligente conectados al Centro de Control, Comando, Comunicaciones, Cómputo, Coordinación e Inteligencia (C5i), con tecnología que permite detectar movimientos y realizar acercamientos en tiempo real.
A estas acciones se sumó la capacitación de 58 operadores del C5i en manejo de crisis y protocolos de atención.
La estrategia de seguridad implementada en Puebla también permitió reforzar el combate al robo de hidrocarburos.
Sánchez González explicó que, tras la explosión registrada el 4 de junio de 2026, autoridades de los tres niveles de gobierno fortalecieron las acciones coordinadas para prevenir y reducir los delitos relacionados con el robo de combustible.
Informó que, del 4 de junio al 16 de agosto, las fuerzas de seguridad recuperaron 178 mil 370 litros de combustible, aseguraron 34 vehículos y 10 contenedores, además de detener a 11 personas presuntamente relacionadas con este delito.
Asimismo, las autoridades inhabilitaron tres tomas clandestinas como parte de los operativos implementados durante ese periodo.

5 LATAM Scania en Expotransporte: récord de ventas, nuevos motores Super y más protagonismo del GNC
Scania en Expotransporte: récord de ventas, nuevos motores Super y más protagonismo del GNC
2026-08-19
Scania volvió a decir presente en Expotransporte después de 15 años de ausencia, con una propuesta que reunió buena parte de su actual portfolio de productos y servicios. La marca exhibió en el predio desde camiones propulsados a GNC y las nuevas motorizaciones Super de 11 litros hasta la edición limitada desarrollada por los 50 años de Scania Argentina. La propuesta se completó con unidades destinadas a aplicaciones fuera de ruta y un bitren, mostrando la amplitud de la oferta disponible para distintos segmentos.
En diálogo con Camiones y Buses, Juan Pablo Cagnola, Area Manager de Minería y Off Road de Scania Argentina, destacó la importancia del regreso de la compañía a una de las principales exposiciones del sector y el movimiento registrado durante la primera jornada. Además de los vehículos exhibidos, la presencia de la marca incluyó propuestas vinculadas con posventa, contratos de mantenimiento y Scania Credit, que durante la feria ofreció alternativas de financiación con tasas preferenciales y promociones especiales para servicios y paquetes de repuestos. A su vez, analizó la evolución de los camiones a GNC y el impacto que tendrá la reciente modificación normativa, además de anticipar algunas novedades para los próximos años en materia de buses.
¿Qué significa para Scania estar presentes acá en Expotransporte?
La verdad que es un honor estar nuevamente. Hacía 15 años no estábamos en Expotransporte, así que estar presente en este tipo de expo a nosotros nos encanta y estamos muy contentos de volver. Lo que lo que se está viendo en este primer día es mucho movimiento de gente, mucha concurrencia, así que es muy lindo estar de nuevo por acá.
¿Cuáles son los productos que están en exposición?
Hoy está en exposición básicamente todo el portfolio de Scania, o sea, para el que venga o que pueda visitar la feria se va a encontrar desde camiones a gas, los nuevos camiones con motores 11 litros Super, también van a encontrar incluso hasta la edición limitada que sacamos este año por los 50 años de Scania Argentina y afuera del pabellón también tenemos vehículos en exposición, vehículos de fuera de ruta, hay un bitren incluso, así que bueno, una gama bastante amplia de lo que es el line up de Scania.
Además de la gama de productos, Scania últimamente viene haciendo mucho hincapié en lo que es posventa, contratos de mantenimiento y en Scania Credit, ¿Qué podés comentar al respecto?
Sí, obviamente no solamente tenemos los productos que estamos viendo en exposición, sino también tenemos a todo el equipo de lo que es posventa, de servicios y también de Scania Credit. Particularmente en lo que es Scania Credit incluso tenemos financiaciones exclusivas para lo que es la expo, financiaciones con tasas preferenciales justamente para poder aprovechar esta presencia nuestra, También hay promociones en lo que es servicios para paquetes de repuestos y paquetes de mantenimiento.
¿Qué balance podés hacer del año para la marca?
Bueno, ha sido un año muy positivo realmente. El año pasado por ejemplo hicimos un récord histórico en lo que es venta, en todos los segmentos tanto de servicios como lo que es de unidades, terminamos el año pasado con más de 3.000 camiones vendidos y este año, si bien todavía el año no termina, estamos próximos a repetirlo, sobre todo en lo que es venta. Creemos que vamos a llegar un promedio también de 3.000 camiones que, en la historia de Scania en 50 años, son los únicos dos años que hemos llegado a esos volúmenes, así que a grandes rangos se podría decir que son dos años súper buenos para Scania, y estamos muy contentos obviamente, con mucho trabajo también porque hay mucho por hacer, pero bueno, dos años súper en lo que es la venta principalmente de unidades, en lo que es servicios también y en la parte financiera.
¿Cómo viene, justamente hablando de producto, la aceptación de nueva línea de motores Super?
A Super lo lanzamos hace unos años, hoy la verdad que ya te diría que es por lejos el motor más vendido que tenemos, sobre todo con la incorporación ya de lo que es el 11 litros.
El P350 Super lo tenemos en 350 y en 390 CV, hoy acá en Argentina, en lo que es 11 litros, después obviamente tenemos los de 13. El 350 lo lanzamos nosotros en Expoagro ya a principio de año, allá por marzo, y la verdad que empezaron a llegar ya hace un par de meses a Argentina, en muy buen número y la verdad que los resultados que están teniendo son bárbaros en lo que es consumo, pero también lo están acompañando mucho las ventas.
Realmente es un camión que se posiciona en una zona que por ahí nosotros no estábamos tan presentes, comúnmente Scania por ahí está presente generalmente en unidades de más potencia, entonces poder ubicarnos en ese nicho donde capaz no estábamos tan fuertes va a estar dando muy buenos resultados por esto, muchos clientes nuevos sobre todo que están pudiendo llegar al primer Scania, muchos clientes que por ahí vienen de otras marcas también a probar Scania por primera vez y nada, es Super, todo lo que es el tren de fuerza, caja, diferenciales… entonces los consumos que le dan al cliente y la eficiencia, la disponibilidad, son realmente muy buenos.
Pasando a Green Efficiency, Scania fue una de las pioneras en impulsar la venta de camiones y buses a GNC, ¿Qué repercusiones están teniendo?
Hace poquito se hizo la aprobación de lo que era el anexo R, creo que es un punto crítico o un punto de inflexión para lo que es camiones a GNC, más o menos para resumir lo que es el anexo R, además de nuevas configuraciones, permite a los camiones GNC ampliar, particularmente a estos con suspensión neumática adelante y neumáticos súper anchos, cambiar la capacidad de carga en 2 toneladas. Esas 2 toneladas ayudan a compensar justamente lo que es el peso extra que tiene el camión a GNC, además de que se amplía al largo total un metro más para compensar en lo que es este camión un poco más largo de distancia entre ejes, y eso básicamente pone en iguales condiciones al camión diésel contra el

7 LATAM The Battery War Intensifies: BYD and CATL Vie for Solid-State Leadership With Nearly Identical Timelines
The Battery War Intensifies: BYD and CATL Vie for Solid-State Leadership With Nearly Identical Timelines
2026-08-18
CATLandBYD, China’s two largest battery and electric vehicle makers, have escalated their rivalry in recent weeks over who will dominateall-solid-state batteries—a game-changing technology that promiseshigher energy density, lower weight, and improved safetyby swapping liquid electrolytes for solid materials.
Both companies have set2027as the target for starting small-scale pilot production. That virtually identical timeline underscores just how fast this industrial race is accelerating, keeping the entire industry on high alert.
The announcements came just days apart:CATLshared its plans with investors on July 29, whileBYDhad six new patents published by theChina National Intellectual Property Administration (CNIPA)on July 31, 2026.
Also of interest |General Motors Accelerates Electrification and Hybrid Strategy in South America with Historic Investment
CATL, the world’s largest EV battery manufacturer, officially confirmed its goal of beginning small-scale production of solid-state cells in2027.
The company plans to launch pilot lines at its Yibin facility with roughly2 GWhof capacity, where it will start manufacturing small batches of cells to validate industrial processes, evaluate real-world performance, and analyze production costs.
However,CATLhas been careful to temper expectations about immediate commercialization.Robin Zeng Yuqunstated that solid-state technology is currently atLevel 4on a nine-point Technology Readiness Level (TRL) scale—meaning it is still in lab validation and prototype development.
Wu Kaiset the target of reachingLevels 7 or 8by2027, which would allow for testing much closer to real-world driving conditions and justify launching the first pilot lines. The company also indicated that large-scale production—equivalent to roughlyone million vehicles—won’t happen before2030.
On the technical front,CATLhas begun pilot production of solid-state cells with an energy density of500 Wh/kg—roughly double what today’s best conventional lithium-ion batteries deliver. The company is developing sulfide-based solid electrolyte technology, and its patents reveal advancements in anode materials that include layers with a substrate material, a cobalt-rich material, and a coating containing an electrolyte salt.
Another patent describes a double-layer cathode composite structure that blends monocrystalline and polycrystalline materials to balance structural stability with rate capability (the ability to maintain charge under high demand).
The tight alignment of their timelines makes it clear just how fiercely competition is accelerating inside China to dominate the next generation of batteries. The fact thatCATLandBYDmoved almost simultaneously underscores the competitive pressure in the Chinese market and the urgency not to fall behind in a technology considered strategic for the future of EVs.
One of the biggest hurdles to bringing solid-state batteries to the mass market isprice. BothCATLandBYDbelieve the first solid-state cells will carry significantly higher production costs than conventional lithium-ion cells, so initial commercial applications will be reserved for high-end vehicles.
Robin Zengnoted that early integration will be limited to automotive platforms priced above250,000 yuan(roughly $37,000 USD).BYD, while not offering concrete figures, indicated its first solid-state batteries will also debut in its premium models first.
Both companies agree that mass production—and the cost reductions needed to reach lower-priced vehicles—won’t materialize until after2030. By then, the market should have enough volume to justify the investments required to scale the technology.
The big question on everyone’s mind is who will get there first.CATLstarts with an edge in global scale and R&D firepower, butBYDhas the advantage of being both a vehicle and battery manufacturer, allowing it to integrate advancements into its own models much faster.
What’s clear is that the competition betweenCATLandBYDis accelerating innovation in a sector that, for years, promised breakthroughs without committing to specific dates. Now, with concrete industrial timelines, published patents, and clear readiness targets, the race for thesolid-state batteryhas entered its most decisive phase.
TheLatam Mobility 2026 Tourwill arrive inSantiago, Chile, on August 25, bringing together experts and strategic players to further strengthen the sustainable mobility ecosystem in the region.
The tour will end inMexico City on October 12 and 13, alongside theClimate Economy Forum, in a meeting that will bring together sector leaders to continue driving the transition toward more efficient, sustainable, low‑emission transportation systems in Latin America.

12 LATAM EU pone en la mira a la carga de transbordo, acusa a 40 países de evadir aranceles
EU pone en la mira a la carga de transbordo, acusa a 40 países de evadir aranceles
2026-08-14
Estados Unidos identificó a más de 40 países con un riesgo elevado de transbordo ilegal, entre ellos México, una práctica de enviar mercancías a través de jurisdicciones con aranceles más bajos antes de que ingresen al mercado estadounidense.
En un informe denominado, “La gran estafa del transbordo”, Estados Unidos expone que el transbordo ilegal puede implicar cambiar etiquetas, reempaquetar, volver a facturar, realizar un procesamiento menor, declarar falsamente el país de origen para evadir aranceles.
Te recomendamos leer:Estados Unidos propone aranceles a remolques de México y China
Dicho informe considera en un primer nivel a los países y bloques comerciales que concentran grandes volúmenes absolutos de mercancías vinculadas con China, pero que también cuentan con bases industriales diversificadas y grandes plataformas de exportación hacia Estados Unidos.
En esta lista están Canadá, la Unión Europea, India, Israel, Japón, México, Corea del Sur y Taiwán.
De acuerdo con el informe de la Casa Blanca,este tipo de prácticas pueden suponer recursos entre 40,000 millones y 303,000 millones de dólares anuales, según estimaciones de organismos gubernamentales y del sector privado, que utilizan diferentes bases de datos, metodologías, filtros de productos y definiciones de transbordo ilegal.
Te recomendamos leer:Caen exportaciones de la industria automotriz a Estados Unidos en marzo
No obstante, de acuerdo con el informe de la Casa Blanca, bajo un escenario central de 75,000 millones de dólares anuales de transbordo ilegal, implica aproximadamente 450,000 empleos desplazados.
Así como entre 113,000 y 150,000 millones de dólares de reducción anual del PIB; y entre 19,000 y 26,000 millones de dólares de pérdidas de ingresos federales.
La Casa Blanca destacó la práctica de China  de utilizar estos terceros países para realizar procesamiento menor; cambio de etiquetas; reempaque; refacturación; o cambios de ruta.
Cuando un producto chino sujeto a un arancel estadounidense elevado puede ser enviado a través de un país con una tasa arancelaria menor, la diferencia se convierte en una fuente de ganancias.
Entre la red para la carga de transbordo que considera Estados Unidosse ubican centros de acabado; plataformas logísticas; corredores de procesamiento; operadores de zonas francas; y centros de reexportación.

Kia Ushers In a New Era With the Launch of Its First 100% Electric Vehicle Built in Mexico
2026-08-13
Kia Méxicohas officiallyunveiledtheKia EV3 2027, marking the South Korean brand’s first 100% electric vehicle to be built on Mexican soil.
This launch represents a major milestone in the company’s global electrification strategy, positioning thePesquería plantin Nuevo León as a production hub for EVs destined for both the domestic and export markets.
TheKia EV3 2027is the first battery electric vehicle (BEV) thatKiahas assembled in Mexico. Production kicked off on August 4 at the Pesquería facility, which underwent a comprehensive modernization and retooling process to accommodate a dedicated line for this electric SUV.
The automaker has earmarked$649 millionfor the project, running through 2028. That investment covers not only plant upgrades but also supply chain enhancements, charging infrastructure, renewable energy, and water recycling initiatives tied to the industrial complex.
During the presentation,Andy Kimhighlighted the significance of the launch for both the company and the country. “Every ‘Hecho en México‘ vehicle that rolls off our plant floor reflects the world-class talent, passion, and dedication of our Mexican workforce, demonstrating to the world what we can achieve together,” the executive stated.
Also of interest|Electromobility Investments Soar 146% in Mexico and Set a New Record in the Automotive Sector
TheKia EV3 2027is a subcompact SUV measuring 4.3 meters in length with a 2.68-meter wheelbase. It is built on the 400-volte-GMPelectric platform—a dedicated EV architecture that optimizes interior space, improves energy efficiency, and integrates advanced safety and connectivity systems.
The EV3’s electric motor delivers201 horsepowerand209 lb-ftof torque. When it comes to range, the model is offered in two configurations:
On charging, the 58.3 kWh version can go from 10% to 80% in approximately29 minutes, while the 81.4 kWh version does the same in roughly31 minutes.
TheKia EV3 2027is the first model in the brand’s Mexican lineup to feature theKia Connectplatform—a connectivity suite that provides access to Over-The-Air (OTA) remote updates,Digital Key 2.0, and the newKia Oneapp.
The vehicle’s technology package includes a segmented panoramic display nearly30 inchesacross (comprising three modules: a 12.3-inch cluster, a 5.3-inch climate control screen, and a 12.3-inch infotainment system).
The SXL version adds aHarman Kardonpremium audio system with 8 speakers, ventilated and heated leather seats, a panoramic sliding sunroof, and LED projection cube headlights.
On the safety front, the EV3 incorporates Advanced Driver Assistance Systems (ADAS) and features aNACS(North American Charging Standard) port, making it compatible withTesla‘s charging network.
The EV3’s design followsKia‘s “Opposites United” philosophy, with geometric lines and clean surfaces that give it a bold, futuristic look. The Star Map daytime running lights and the signature Tiger Face grille reinforce its distinctive visual identity.
The$649 millioninvestment in the Pesquería plant not only modernizes the facility but also delivers a significant boost to employment and the supply chain. The project is expected to generate500 new direct jobsin 2026 and an additional1,500 by 2030.
The Mexico-built EV3 will initially have about27% local content, a figure that is expected to climb as more regional suppliers are brought into the program. High-value components, such as battery packs, will arrive from Asia initially and gradually shift to suppliers located closer to Pesquería.
The investment program also includes funding for public and semi-public charging infrastructure along key corridors in Mexico, tied toKia‘s dealer network, as well as renewable energy and water recycling projects at the plant.
TheKia EV3 2027is now available throughKia México‘s dealer network and viaMercadoLibre—an unprecedented sales strategy for the brand. Launch prices are set at689,700 Mexican pesosfor the EX version and767,700 pesosfor the SXL version.
Pre-sales are already open, and the first Mexican-built units will begin exporting to the United States in the second half of 2026.
The company noted that electrified vehicles currently account for25%ofKia‘s global sales, with a target of reaching52%by 2030.
The design and technology of theKia EV3have already earned global recognition, including prestigious honors such as theiF Design Award 2025, theRed Dot Design Award 2025, and the2025 World Car of the Yearaward.

Con esta estrategia, Guanajuato prevé alcanzar 8 mil millones de dólares de inversión acumulada y generar 50 mil nuevos empleos vinculados con los sectores considerados estratégicos para 2030.
Durante la presentación, García Muñoz Ledo anunció la llegada de 17 nuevos proyectos de inversión por 2 mil 558 millones de dólares, que representan la creación de 3 mil 567 empleos comprometidos.
Con estas inversiones, la actual administración acumulará 76 proyectos por 6 mil 406.55 millones de dólares y 18 mil 811 empleos, equivalente al 80% de la meta sexenal de inversión.
Los nuevos proyectos abarcan diversos sectores, entre ellos el automotriz, minería, construcción, centros de datos, desarrollos inmobiliarios y energía.

16 LATAM Isuzu lleva la movilidad más allá del transporte con «Rodar por Ayudar» 2026
Isuzu lleva la movilidad más allá del transporte con «Rodar por Ayudar» 2026
2026-08-12
Como parte de su responsabilidad social y con el objetivo de seguir contribuyendo al bienestar de las comunidades mexicanas y devolver parte de lo que el país le ha brindado a la compañía durante más de dos décadas de presencia,Isuzu Motors de México anunció la edición 2026 de «Rodar por Ayudar».
El programa, que se llevará a cabo de agosto a noviembre de este año,contempla la entregas de sillas de ruedas en distintas entidades del país en coordinación con la Red Nacional de Concesionarios Isuzu,lo que permitirá llevar el programa a distintas regiones del país y generar un impacto directo en las comunidades donde la marca tiene presencia.
Te recomendamos leer:Utilidades de Isuzu crecen a doble dígito en su primer trimestre fiscal
Isuzu explicó que este año cuentan con el apoyo de la Fundación Bertha O. de Osete, I.A.P.,institución con más de tres décadas de experiencia y más de 100,000 personas beneficiadas en México a través de programas que promueven la movilidad, la autonomía y la inclusión.
Uno de los aspectos que distingue el trabajo de la fundación es que las sillas de ruedas y otros dispositivos de movilidad son fabricados en México,utilizando componentes de fácil acceso para su mantenimiento y reparación, incluyendo piezas compatibles con bicicletas convencionales. Esto facilita que los usuarios puedan mantener sus dispositivos en condiciones adecuadas de funcionamiento y prolongar su vida útil.
Asimismo, la labor de la fundación tiene un impacto que va más allá de la entrega de los dispositivos, ya que brinda oportunidades laborales a personas con discapacidad que participan en la fabricación de estos productos, generando un modelo que vincula movilidad, inclusión laboral y desarrollo social.
Te recomendamos leer:Isuzu fortalece su portafolio en México con el lanzamiento de la pick up D-MAX
«El año pasado iniciamos Rodar por Ayudar con la convicción de agradecer a México la confianza que ha depositado en nuestra marca durante dos décadas», comentóHiroshi Ikewaga, presidente y director general de Isuzu Motors de México.
«Este año queremos dar continuidad a ese compromiso a través de una acción que tiene un significado muy especial para nosotros:contribuir a que más personas puedan contar con las herramientas necesarias para desplazarse con mayor independencia. Para Isuzu, la movilidad no solo significa transportar personas o mercancías; también representa una oportunidad para transformar vidas y generar un impacto positivo en la sociedad», añadió Ikewaga.
El programa «Rodar por Ayudar» nació en 2025 como una iniciativa conmemorativa por el 20 aniversario de Isuzu Motors de México, con el propósito de apoyar a diversas instituciones de asistencia social en distintas regiones del país y devolver, a través de este esfuerzo, parte de lo que México le ha brindado a la compañía durante más de dos décadas de presencia.
A partir de la experiencia obtenida durante la primera edición y de las necesidades identificadas,este año Isuzu decidió enfocar sus acciones en el acceso a dispositivos que favorezcan la movilidad de las personas.
Con las acciones de «Rodar por Ayudar» 2026,Isuzu Motors de Méxicoreafirma su compromiso de contribuir al desarrollo de las comunidades mexicanasmediante proyectos que promueven la inclusión y generan un impacto positivo.

17 LATAM 20 Million Electric Vehicles Sold Worldwide and an Underlying Problem: Can Latin America Support Smart Charging?
20 Million Electric Vehicles Sold Worldwide and an Underlying Problem: Can Latin America Support Smart Charging?
2026-08-12
During the webinar“Plan México 2030: Nearshoring, Manufacturing and the Smart Charging Ecosystem,”organized byLatam Mobility, three industry leaders agreed on a stark reality:electromobility in the region is advancing at a rapid pace, but charging infrastructure and grid capacity are failing to keep pace.
The event brought togetherMaría Cabeza, Country Manager ofSynergEVin Mexico;Fadwa Kingsbury, Director of Operations atAmpcontrol; andKathy Ardila, Commercial Manager ofInvest in Latamas moderator, to highlight the challenges and opportunities shaping the energy transition across the continent.
You may also be interested |BYD, CATL and FAW will lead the revolution of the ultra-fast charger: the time will pass in seconds
The world closed out 2025 with a historic record in electric vehicle sales. According to the latestGlobal EV Outlookreport from theInternational Energy Agency (IEA), global sales surpassed 20 million units for the first time, representing more than a quarter of the global new car market.
This growth, which has remained steady despite economic uncertainty, positions electric vehicles as a mass phenomenon rather than a passing trend.
Projections for 2026 are equally optimistic. The IEA revised its forecasts upward, estimating that electric vehicles will represent 29% of global car sales—equivalent to approximately 23 million units.
Latin America, with growth exceeding 100% in sales during the first half of 2026, has emerged as a key engine of global growth. Mexico and Brazil, along with Colombia, Uruguay, and Argentina, lead this regional dynamism.
In this context of exponential growth, Mexico positions itself as a strategic player thanks to the nearshoring phenomenon, which has attracted foreign investment for the manufacturing of electric vehicles and auto parts. However, during the webinar it became clear thatvehicle production is not advancing at the same pace as the deployment of smart charging infrastructure.
María Cabezawas emphatic: “Mexico is setting the course in production, but growth in charging infrastructure isn’t keeping pace. For these vehicles to be sold, users need the certainty and peace of mind of being able to charge.” TheSynergEVexecutive highlighted that while Mexico occupies a key place alongside Brazil in regional manufacturing, the central challenge lies in balancing the scales to ensure vehicle supply is met with a reliable charging network.
Fadwa Kingsburyechoed this view, focusing on grid capacity: “Even if there are thousands of vehicles, the problem is going to be grid capacity. The big challenge we are going to face is how to handle all that extra load.”
TheAmpcontroldirector warned that Mexico can learn from Europe’s experiences, where countries like Germany and the Netherlands face similar challenges and have begun integrating batteries, solar panels, and generators to sustain the demand of electric fleets.
Kathy Ardilaemphasized the opportunity that renewable energies represent to overcome the limitations of the current grid. “The main challenge, not only in Mexico but throughout Latin America, is the capacity of the installed grid, which is not technologically advanced enough. That is where the implementation of solar projects and hybrid solutions plays a fundamental role, especially in non-interconnected locations.”
Beyond the physical connection between the vehicle and the charger, the panelists agreed thatthe future of electromobility lies in smart energy management through software, data, and artificial intelligence (AI).
María Cabezaexplained thatSynergEVoffers 360-degree solutions that integrate hardware and software to optimize energy use according to the user type: residential, fleets, or charging hubs. “It’s not just about the charger working; it’s about financial sustainability. Energy must be used based on smart services, considering electricity rates and consumption peaks,” she detailed.
Fadwa Kingsburydelved intoAmpcontrol‘s approach—a platform that centralizes all data in a single software suite: from communication with chargers via the OCPP protocol to vehicle telemetry, battery state of charge, and route planning. “Bringing all the data together allows for efficient planning. It’s not just about knowing that there are peak rates, but that those rates coincide with the times when vehicles are in the depot,” she explained.
The executive added that AI allows predictive identification of failures and resolves 99% of issues before they occur, representing aquantum leapcompared to traditional maintenance models.
Both experts agreed that operational flexibility is just as important as technology.“Depots and fleets are used to operating a certain way with traditional vehicles, but electric vehicles require a new mode of operation. If people don’t adapt, the tools are useless,”Kingsbury warned.
The webinar also served as a platform to make industry announcements.María Cabezarevealed thatSynergEV, a US-based company, will install its first assembly plant in Mexico in early 2027. This decision, she explained, responds to the need to streamline delivery times and offer more efficient after-sales service—two of the main pain points in the Mexican market, especially in a context of tariff and customs challenges. “We are happy because we will be able to respond more quickly to our customers,” she stated.
Fadwa Kingsburyhighlighted thatAmpcontrolis focused on integrating multiple energy sources—solar panels, batteries, and buildings—to coordinate all energy on a single platform. “We want operators to be able to add more vehicles without needing to install more chargers, making the most of existing infrastructure,” she explained. The executive emphasized the importance of long-term planning, considering not only current demand but also the projected growth of electric fleets.
Meanwhile,Plan México 2030—which includes the goal of increasing the production of electric vehicles assembled in the country by 10%—was another central theme of the
Latam Mobility
18 LATAM BYD, CATL, and FAW Lead the Ultra-Fast Charging Revolution: Time is Now Measured in Seconds
BYD, CATL, and FAW Lead the Ultra-Fast Charging Revolution: Time is Now Measured in Seconds
2026-08-11
The Chinese automotive industry has entered a new era. For years, the charging time of an electric vehicle was measured in tens of minutes—a major barrier that kept many drivers tied to diesel and gasoline.
In 2026, that reality is firmly in the rearview mirror. Chinese manufacturers are no longer competing to reduce charging time from one hour to half an hour;they are competing for seconds.
The race for ultra-fast charging has turned China into the global laboratory for electromobility, with batteries capable of going from 10% to 80% capacity inless than four minutesand charging systems reaching power levels of1,500 kW—matching electric charging times to those of conventional refueling.
The message is unmistakable:China no longer measures charging time in minutes; it measures it in seconds.And what is happening in the Asian giant is not an isolated phenomenon but rather the announcement of a paradigm shift that will redefine electric mobility on a global scale.
You may also be interested in |Researchers discover that “constant pressure” could double the lifespan of lithium batteries
The starting gun was fired in March 2026 byBYD. The Chinese manufacturer unveiled itssecond-generation Blade Battery (Blade Battery 2.0)alongside itsFlash Chargingsystem—a charging platform that delivers1,500 kW per charging port.
The results are extraordinary: with this combination, a vehicle can charge from 10% to 70% in justfive minutesand reach97% capacity in nine minutes. Even in extreme conditions of -30°C, charging from 20% to 97% is completed in approximately twelve minutes.
The battery, based on lithium-iron-phosphate (LFP) technology, has increased its energy density by 5% compared to the first generation, enablingranges exceeding 1,000 kilometersunder the Chinese CLTC test cycle.
ButBYDhas not limited itself to battery technology. The company has deployed a large-scale infrastructure strategy: by March 2026, it had already installed4,239 Flash Charging stationsacross China, with the goal of reaching20,000 stationsby the end of the year.
In the company’s own words, this is “Flash Charging China, changing the world”—a statement of intent that reflects the manufacturer’s ambition to lead the global energy transition.
Battery giantCATLhas not been left behind. At its “Super Tech Day” in April 2026, the company unveiled thethird generation of its Shenxing ultra-fast charging battery, a technology that has set new industry benchmarks.
The third-generation Shenxing offers a charging rate equivalent to10Cand reaches peaks of15C(compared to 1C or 2C for a conventional battery), meaning it absorbs charge at a speedten times higherthan a standard battery.
The charging times are nothing short of revolutionary: from 10% to 35% in just1 minute, from 10% to 80% in3 minutes and 44 seconds, and from 10% to 98% in just6 minutes and 27 seconds.
Even in extreme conditions of -30°C, the battery maintains exceptional performance, expanding its applicability to cold-climate regions.CATLannounced that mass production of this battery is scheduled for the fourth quarter of 2026.
ButCATLhas not stopped there. In July of this year, the company launched theTianxing II (Tectrans II)battery for light commercial vehicles, with an 8C charging rate that allows reaching 80% in6 minutes and 48 secondsand full charge in8 minutes and 56 seconds.
This battery, designed specifically for the logistics sector, reduces the internal resistance of its cells by50% below the industry averageand offers a10-year or 1-million-kilometer warranty.CATLplans to install4,000 ultra-fast charging stationsin nearly 190 Chinese cities during 2026, with a medium-term goal of10,000 stations.
State-ownedFAW Group, through its luxury brandHongqiand in collaboration with battery manufacturerLishen Battery, has achieved what may be the most impressive record to date. In performance tests conducted at 25°C, the jointly developed battery managed to charge from 10% to 70% in3 minutes and 41 seconds—the fastest time recorded so far for a Chinese passenger EV battery.
The battery reaches a peak charging rate of12C, surpassingGeely‘s Golden Brick battery, which achieved the same range in 4 minutes and 22 seconds. Additionally, Hongqi’s battery charges from 10% to 97% in8 minutes and 3 seconds, ahead of Geely’s 8 minutes and 42 seconds.
The secret to this speed lies in several key innovations:
Thermal management is one of the biggest challenges in 12C charging. Hongqi’s battery incorporates an intelligent uniform-temperature liquid cooling system that maintains a temperature difference across the entire battery pack ofless than 3°Cduring fast charging, along with a real-time adaptive charging strategy that monitors temperature rise and dynamically adjusts the power curve.
Geely, through itsLynk & Cobrand, was one of the first to demonstrate that charging in a matter of minutes was possible. ItsGolden Brickbattery achieves charging from 10% to 70% in4 minutes and 22 seconds—a time that, although surpassed by the latest announcements, remains extraordinary. In certified tests, the battery reached a peak charging power of1,093 kW.
Battery manufacturerGotion High-Techjoined the competition with itssecond-generation G-Instant battery, unveiled in May 2026. This battery, also based on LFP technology, supports a maximum charge of12Cand can charge from 10% to 80% in just4.9 minutes, and from 10% to 97% in8.7 minutes.Gotionis in the mass production preparation phase, with a projected launch for the first quarter of 2027.
State-owned manufacturerDongfengalso entered the race with a1,500 kW per-gun charging system, matching the capacity announced byBYD. The system architecture starts from a main unit with 1.2 MW, designed to support configurations that can reach 2.4 MW of combined power.Dongfengis already working on a new generation of chargers that will incorporate energy storage, with a family of equipment covering power levels from 720 kW to 2.4 MW.

19 LATAM General Motors Accelerates Electrification and Hybrid Strategy in South America with Historic Investment
General Motors Accelerates Electrification and Hybrid Strategy in South America with Historic Investment
2026-08-07
General Motorshas earmarked a total investment package of R$10.5 billion (approximately USD 1.9 billion) for the period between 2024 and 2028 in Brazil, with the goal of reshaping its operations across South America and accelerating the shift toward electrified mobility.
Of that total, R$5.5 billion has been specifically directed to operations in the state of São Paulo, covering the development of flexible hybrid engines and the comprehensive modernization of industrial facilities.
This financial commitment reinforces the automaker’s long-term dedication to the region and directly responds to the surging demand for electrified vehicles in the country, where they already make up nearly 16% of new registrations.
Thomas Owsianski, President and Managing Director of GM South America, emphasized that the investment expands the company’s capacity to develop highly competitive vehicles while fast-tracking the adoption of new propulsion technologies.
You may also be interested in |Chile: Budget constraints drive bus retrofitting as an alternative to the traditional fleet renewal model
The company is currently in the final phase of development and homologation for its new hybrid propulsion systems.Fábio Rua, Vice President of General Motors for South America, confirmed that the new mechanical assemblies are now in the final stages of development—indicating that the engineering process is highly advanced and ready for production.
Similarly,Thomas Owsianskinoted during the announcement of the five millionth vehicle produced at the Gravataí plant that the hybrids are now deep into the final homologation process.
The first hybrid vehicles developed specifically for the Brazilian market will adopt 48-volt mild hybrid (MHEV) technology, paired with 1.0 and 1.2-liter turbo flex engines. This system will deliver noticeable gains in fuel efficiency and lower emissions without requiring radical structural overhauls, making it a practical and accessible solution for the region.
AlthoughGeneral Motorshas not officially disclosed the exact launch order, theTrackerandMontanastand out as the natural choices to debut the domestic hybrid system. Both vehicles share the GEM platform, utilize the 1.2-liter three-cylinder turbo flex engine, and are produced at the São Caetano do Sul plant—a facility that concentrates a significant portion of the investments earmarked for electrification.
In parallel, theChevrolet Onixfamily—including the hatchback and Onix Plus sedan versions—is also expected to be among the first models to incorporate this new technology.
TheChevrolet Tracker, one of the brand’s best-selling vehicles in the country and throughout the region, will lead this electrification push alongside the renewed 2026Onix. Both models are scheduled to be unveiled at a special event in Brazil celebrating GM’s 100 years of operations in the country.
General Motors‘ strategy to revamp its entry-level portfolio in South America is already paying off, delivering a resounding commercial victory. The newChevrolet Sonicshattered all initial expectations by recording 14,000 units sold during its very first week of availability at dealerships in Brazil.
This figure not only underscores the model’s strong appeal among consumers seeking a modern, efficient, and affordable vehicle, but it also sets a new launch benchmark for the brand in the region.The early success confirms that the bet on refreshing the entry-level lineup is fully justified.
The Sonic’s initial momentum is just the opening act of a much broader commercial rollout.Thomas Owsianskiofficially confirmed that in the coming months, the company will introduce additional versions of the model, designed to cover different price segments and customer needs.
But the project’s ambition extends well beyond Brazilian borders: the executive confirmed that theChevrolet Sonicwill be exported to strategic markets across the region, including Argentina, Colombia, Peru, and Uruguay, as well as other nations in the area. This decision reinforces Brazil’s role as the nerve center for production and development across the automaker’s entire South American operations.
In parallel with the commercial launch, the Gravataí plant, located in the state of Rio Grande do Sul, has reached a major industrial milestone. Since it began operations in the year 2000, the factory has maintained a steady production pace, allowing it to hit the 5 million assembled vehicles mark.
This production milestone carries special symbolic weight, as the exact unit that marked the record of 5 million vehicles produced was the newChevrolet Sonicitself. This coincidence directly connects the plant’s industrial legacy with the brand’s commercial future.
General Motors‘ commitment to hybrids in South America is a direct response to markets where fully electric vehicles still face infrastructure and cost barriers. The company has opted for a gradual, multi-layered approach that combines mild hybrids (MHEV), plug-in hybrids (PHEV), and pure electrics (BEV) to offer a truly diversified portfolio.
Chevroletplans to introduce roughly four models with electrified powertrains in the Brazilian market during 2026. These include theCaptiva PHEV(plug-in hybrid), which will complement the all-electric version of the SUV, as well as hybrid variants of theTrackerandMontana.
The strategy also includes local production of electric vehicles, such as theChevrolet Spark EUV, which is already assembled at the Comexport PACE plant in Ceará.
Furthermore,General Motors‘ investment plan encompasses adaptations to assembly lines, local component development, engine updates, and thorough preparation of the supplier chain for the new electrified systems. The company maintains plants in São Caetano do Sul (producing the Tracker, Montana, and Spin), São José dos Campos (manufacturing the S10, Trailblazer, engines, and transmissions), and Mogi das Cruzes (stamped components).

5 APAC Market enjoys jolly July of deliveries
Market enjoys jolly July of deliveries
2026-08-20
The past month may have seen the local bus and coach industry turn its focus to the Sydney Showgrounds and a stellar Bus & Coach Expo, but it sure didn’t stop the constant stream of deliveries from rolling through. The latest Australian bus and coach deliveries data, brought to you byABC,saw 167 units recorded in July.
The higher numbers saw the leaders in the chassis sector flex their muscles – none greater than Volvo courtesy of 46 deliveries. Scania came in second (29) narrowly ahead of a surging BYD (27), while Yutong’s mix of 12 heavy and three light bus deliveries saw it finish fourth. Not far behind was Zhongtong with 10 heavy and one light bus delivery. Closely trailing was Custom Denning (nine), BCI (seven), King Long (seven), Odin (six) and BLK (five), while MAN (four) and Challenger (one) wrapped up the market.
The diverse body building market gave up no surprises in July as Volgren recorded 73 deliveries for the month. Irizar sat a fair way back in second with 17 deliveries, with Yutong’s 12 heavy and three light bus deliveries seeing it claim third. Zhongtong’s mix of 10 heavy and one light bus granted it fourth place ahead of Custom Denning (nine), BCI (seven), King Long (seven) and BLK (seven). The final 19 deliveries were split between six brands as the market continued its even trend.
The seating market continued to belong to McConnell through a resounding 85 deliveries for July. The next best was Sege with 31 deliveries, while StyleRide (14) claimed third ahead of Zhongtong (11), Yutong (10), King Long (seven) and Fainsa (nine).
The air-conditioning market is no longer close, with Coachair pulling away from its competitors through 51 July deliveries. The next best was Spheros with 25 units, while Hispacold also stood clear of the peloton courtesy of 24 units, while Cling-Yutong had 15 deliveries. Not far behind were Thermo King (13), Denso (11) and Tongsun (11), while King Long (seven), Konvekta (six) and BCC (three) wrapped up the market.
It was a case of another month and another win to NSW as the state recorded 63 deliveries in July. Queensland was a clear second with 38 deliveries, with Western Australia usurping Victoria 26 v 22 to claim third. South Australia sat behind with 17 deliveries while the Northern Territory recorded a sole unit.
The power source split pulled its way back to a more even keel in July as 70 of the 167 units recorded were zero-emissions. In this market, BYD (27) led the way ahead of an even market that included Custom Denning (nine), Volvo (nine) and Scania (eight). Zhongtong recorded the single hybrid unit, while Volvo (37) and Scania (21) led the way out of 96 diesel deliveries.

2021款沃尔沃XC90，是50万级豪华SUV里把“安全”二字做成信仰的车型，没有之一。在中大型豪华SUV细分市场，它用1500MPa硼钢车身硬扛40吨卡车60km/h正面撞击，全系标配City Safety城市智能安全系统，座舱做到母婴级标准。2021款在售期间限时参考价39.99万元起，叠加至高1万置换补贴和0首付60期金融方案，把纯进口旗舰SUV拉进40万以内。为什么说它是安全天花板？三个维度拆开看。
🛡️ 1500MPa硼钢🚛 扛住40吨卡车撞击👶 母婴级座舱🤖 City Safety全系标配💰 39.99万起
先说被动安全，因为XC90的底气，全在那副“钢筋铁骨”里。车身采用笼式硼钢闭环结构，A柱延伸到C柱是一体成型超长硼钢，关键部位强度达到1500MPa。什么概念？普通乘用车高强度钢大多在800-1000MPa，XC90直接把强度拉升了50%以上——相当于每平方厘米能承受15吨压力。
更硬核的是极限碰撞验证：在超常规测试中，全新XC90正面承受40吨卡车以60km/h撞击，乘员舱未变形，前挡风玻璃完好；随后再遭四吨轻卡以55km/h追尾，2.4米超长一体硼钢扛住冲击，仅A柱轻微变形而生存空间无损，全车侧气帘与预紧安全带协同生效。这就是高速连环追尾的极端情况——普通车在这种场景下大概率被压缩成铁饼，XC90里的人生还空间还在。
细节上，前防撞梁与吸能盒充分溃缩吸能，机舱环抱式上边梁参与能量传导，B柱采用双区双工艺：上半部分刚性保护胸头，下半部分缓冲保护骨盆下肢。所以家里有老人小孩的，XC90的意义不是“豪华”，而是把“万一”变成“不怕”。
撞不坏是底线，不撞才是本事。XC90的主动安全，走的是“预判+干预”全场景路线。全系标配City Safety城市智能安全系统，前部雷达+摄像头昼夜工作，能识别车辆、行人、骑行者，甚至大型动物。大型动物识别碰撞预防功能，车速最高可降低15km/h——在高速和国道遇到突然窜出的牲畜时，这可能就是生与死的距离。
Pilot Assist智能领航辅助，支持0-130km/h全速域自适应弯道居中巡航。车道线清晰、行驶规范的路况下，车辆自己完成跟车、刹车、打方向，长途跑高速右脚能歇着。道路偏离预防与保护系统，提前预警偏离危险并自动干预转向，把事故掐死在摇篮里。核心配置整理如下：
表格：2021款XC90核心安全配置一览（数据来自公开测试及官方资料）
非决胜点，但加分很多。XC90内饰限用6000余种有害物质，达到母婴级标准；CLEANZONE清洁座舱PM2.5过滤效率超95%；座椅由骨科医生参与研发，配合WHIPS头颈保护系统，追尾时降低颈椎损伤风险。无边框后视镜导水槽、仪表台织物包覆这些细节，也在减少雨天视野干扰和反光分心。对孕妇、婴幼儿家庭，这是“隐形刚需”。
💡编辑部核心结论：2021款XC90或许不是最有排面的豪华SUV，但一定是50万价位里最能保命的一台。安全配置全系无差别覆盖，加上39.99万起的限时价格，家庭用户闭眼买错不了。
是的。City Safety、Pilot Assist、笼式硼钢车身等核心安全配置，低配与顶配完全一致。沃尔沃不在安全上做配置高低区隔，这也是它和同级竞品最大的差异点——入门版也拥有完整的安全装备。
真实。2021款XC90在售期内打出限时参考尊享价39.99万元起，这是纯进口旗舰SUV，不是特供版。还可叠加至高1万置换补贴和0首付60期金融政策。但限时活动以当期门店政策为准，建议到店核实。
问题3：2021款XC90和奥迪Q7(配置|询价)、宝马X5(配置|询价)怎么选？
把安全和健康放第一位就选XC90——它经受过40吨卡车撞击测试，座舱是母婴级标准；更看品牌声量和操控感，奥迪Q7、宝马X5可以看，但同价位下它们的主动安全配置很难像XC90这样全系拉满。

成毅逆应援天花板！4000杯奶茶不限量

规模空前：2026年2月10日《两京十五日》杀青现场，成毅自费订购约4000杯奶茶，因门店产能所限最终交付3500杯，用卡车运至横店明清宫苑片场。
诚意细节：奶茶分冷热、多种糖度，杯身印有定制杯贴；立牌明确写着"不限量、不够再买"，粉丝、路人、环卫工人都可领取。
提前筹备：奶茶店透露"几天前接到毅哥的4000杯大单"，店家为此闭店一天专供制作。
长期坚持：从2016年起，成毅便持续以自费方式回馈粉丝支持，被粉丝评价为"从有粉丝基础开始，大大小小吃的喝的一直没断过"。
四季不断：夏日片场自费采购矿泉水、冷饮、西瓜、凉茶，寒冬准备热奶茶和点心，中秋送定制月饼，跨年夜送千杯热饮。
全员覆盖：2024年《赴山海》开机时准备三卡车、上万份定制礼包（防晒套装、酸奶、卸妆膏、洗脸巾等），剧组工作人员与场外粉丝一并分享。
体贴入微：2024年生日当天自费购买冷风机为粉丝降温，并安排无人机字幕致谢。
换位思考：因粉丝聚集可能打扰周边居民，自费采购大米、牛奶登门致歉。
真心换真心：粉丝认为"逆应援不过是成毅对粉丝支持的感谢""物质是心意载体，内核是发自内心对每一位果果的尊重"。

2026-08-20

《两京十五日》杀青（2026年2月）：成毅自费租卡车运送约3500杯奶茶至横店明清宫苑片场，立牌写明“不限量、不够再买”，奶茶分冷热、多种糖度并定制杯贴。

生日片场（2024年5月17日）：横店天气炎热，成毅自费购买多台冷风机摆放在应援点，并安排无人机字幕向粉丝致谢。
贴心考虑周边居民：因粉丝聚集可能打扰当地住户，成毅自费采购大米、牛奶，挨家挨户登门致歉。
关照非粉丝群体：跨年夜及多次活动场外，奶茶同样分给路人、环卫工人、公交司机，不限粉籍、排队即可领取。
防止粉丝中暑：除冷风机外，多次自费采购矿泉水、凉茶、西瓜、粤式盒饭等分给场外守候粉丝。
长期坚持而非一时之举：从《莲花楼》《英雄志》拍摄期到多次跨年晚会，成毅持续以奶茶、热饮、点心回馈粉丝，粉丝评价“在逆应援这个词出现之前他已经在做了”。
以真心换真心：粉丝普遍认为成毅“不善言辞但付诸行动”，正是这份真诚让粉丝粘性极高，形成内娱罕见的“双向奔赴”。

日常投喂：包括烤全羊、800份烤鸡腿、冬至饺子、中秋月饼、马卡龙、冷风机等，品类远超奶茶范畴。

11 APAC Amber adds smart charging for MG, XPeng, and Audi EVs, still no word on more car makers embracing V2G trial
Amber adds smart charging for MG, XPeng, and Audi EVs, still no word on more car makers embracing V2G trial
2026-08-19
Renewable energy services provider Amber Electric says that its automated smart charging feature now supports all MG, Xpeng, and Audi models that have built-in connectivity, although it is yet to say if more car makers are signing on to its expanded V2G trial.
The smart charging feature, known as Amber for Evs, was originally launchedin late 2024, and was focused only on Tesla electric vehicles (EVs), enabling users to access the cheapest wholesale electricity prices to charge their EVs.
Amber for EVssimply requires users to tell the app when the car is needed and how much charge will be necessary, leaving Amber to automatically charge the car with rooftop solar or the cheapest wholesale energy prices – which means that sometimes charging is free, or even pays out to charge.
“The idea is to help customers get as much free charging as possible,” says Chris Thompson, Amber Electric co-CEO. “We are even offering EV owners to get paid to charge.”
Amber has now expanded its offering to more drivers, adding support for all MG, XPeng, and Audi models that have built-in connectivity.
Amber customers pay wholesale electricity prices, which change every five minutes as supply and demand shift across the grid. Amber for EVs starts or stops charging so as to catch the cheapest wholesale price windows.
While Amber for EVs has added support for extra EV models, some functionality is still limited to Tesla EVs, specifically the ability to speed up or slow down charging. However, Amber did promise they are “working” on making this feature available for all EV models supported.
Amber is also conducting the country’s biggest vehicle-to-grid trial, and with the support of ARENA has expanded this from a targeted 50 EVs to up to 1,000. Until now, only BYD has allowed its vehicles to take part, but the company is aiming to expand this to at least four car makers. There is no word yet on which car makers have taken up the offer, if any.
 The Driven

13 APAC トラック運送業界の景況感／運賃・料金水準の改善で26年4～6月は0.5ポイント良化
トラック運送業界の景況感／運賃・料金水準の改善で26年4～6月は0.5ポイント良化
2026-08-19
全日本トラック協会が8月19日に公表した「第134回トラック運送業界の景況感（速報）2026年4月～6月期」によると、トラック運送業界の景況感は前回（1－3月期）から0.5ポイント改善した。
トラック運送業界では、中東情勢悪化に伴う原油高の影響等がマイナス要因として作用した一方、運賃・料金水準の改善による経常損益、営業利益の持ち直したことで、景況感は前回▲29.4から▲28.9へ改善した。
しかし、来期については、輸送数量の減少に加え、原油高に伴う燃料価格高騰への懸念や運賃・料金水準の頭打ちから、景況感は今回▲28.9から▲34.0へ5.1ポイントの悪化を見込んでいる。

15 APAC ASIC minutes list $112m in ACFS trade creditor claims
ASIC minutes list $112m in ACFS trade creditor claims
2026-08-19
ACFS Port Logistics is facing an estimated $112 million in trade creditor claims and a $76 million Australian Taxation Office (ATO) claim, newly filed creditors’ meeting minutes have revealed.
The preliminary figures were tabled at the first meeting of creditors on August 18 and included in minutes filed with ASIC the following day.
The minutes state that the figures are rough initial estimates, with several assets and liabilities yet to be quantified. The chair also noted that creditor claims accepted for voting purposes had not been admitted for dividend purposes, which requires a separate adjudication process.
The statement of position lists $21.3 million in estimated employee entitlements across ACFS Port Logistics, comprising $3.7 million in superannuation, $9 million in annual leave, $7.5 million in long service leave and $1.1 million in rostered days off.
More than 1100 employees are listed as being attached to the company.
The separate $76 million ATO claim includes an estimated $8.9 million superannuation guarantee charge. The minutes do not explain how that amount relates to the $3.7 million superannuation figure listed under employee entitlements.
The document also lists amounts totalling approximately $77.45 million under four secured interests: $45 million for ScotPac, $21 million for Daimler Truck Financial Services, $7.2 million for Octet and $4.25 million for Orix.
The table separately lists $91.8 million in related-party loans, including $43.5 million attributed to ACFS Investments 1, $15.9 million to Terry Tzaneros, $16.7 million to AGS Finco and $15.7 million to AGS World Transport.
The estimated shortfall associated with the company’s properties and leases remains to be determined.
Against those amounts, the minutes list approximately $68 million in receivables and plant and equipment with a written-down value of $87 million. Cash, related-party loans receivable and ACFS Port Logistics’ investment in ACFS Investments 1 have not been quantified.
ACFS Port Logistics operates from five leased premises in NSW, five in Queensland, four in Victoria, two in Western Australia, one in South Australia and one in New Zealand. TZI 1 leases an additional Queensland property.
Despite the scale of the claims, receivers BDO said ACFS had experienced “minimal disruption” since its August 6 appointment and continued to operate as business as usual.
BDO said trading conditions were stabilising and attention would soon turn to a formal sale campaign.
The receivers said they had already been approached by “dozens of parties” interested in acquiring the business or its assets before any formal campaign had begun.
“This unsolicited interest provides confidence that, when launched, the sale campaign will attract strong market engagement and maximise the prospects of a successful outcome,” the BDO statement reads.
At the same time, ACFS shareholders and directors are continuing to pursue a recapitalisation plan for the business.
Administrator Sule Arnautovic told creditors an extension of the administration’s convening period would be sought because the usual 20 business days would not provide enough time to investigate the complex group.
The extension would also give BDO more time to stabilise ACFS, pursue a sale and consider any restructuring proposal.
The administrators are expected to provide creditors with another update after the ATO-led winding-up application, which is scheduled to be heard court on August 21.
Salea is investigating the affairs of the three companies and assessing restructuring options for creditors, while BDO controls the assets and operations of ACFS Port Logistics and ACFS Investments 1 and is preparing for a potential sale.

16 APAC Transit Systems Victoria earns international customer service recognition
Transit Systems Victoria earns international customer service recognition
2026-08-19
Operator Transit Systems Victoria has been awarded one of the highest globally recognised customer health checks.
The Victorian bus operator received its very first International Customer Service Standard and Trustmark (ICSS) audit, showcasing its high standards of service.
The ICSS is administered by the Customer Service Institute of Australia (CSIA) and grades companies on how well it treats its customers and handles problems.
The two-stage process included a detailed self-assessment of customer service systems, processes and documentation followed by a three-day on-site assessment at the West Footscray Depot by an independent ICSS auditor who reviewed records and interviewed staff from drivers to the managing director.
The audit confirmed customer service standards are understood and consistently applied, from leadership through to the frontline. Transit Systems Victoria Managing Director George Konstantopoulos says the result reflects the work undertaken since his appointment in February.
“This is a strong endorsement of the work our people do every day to put customers first,” he says.
“It also supports our commitment to Objective 4 of Victoria’s Bus Plan, a superior customer experience, and we intend to keep building on it.”
The certification comes as Transit Systems Victoria launches its first Customer Charter, setting out the standard of service passengers can expect across safety, reliability, cleanliness and accessibility. Approved by Konstantopoulos, the Charter is built around the company’s COMPASS values and takes effect this month.
Transit Systems Chief Executive Officer Michael McGee congratulated the Victorian team.
“Achieving Implementation-level certification in a first-time assessment is a strong outcome,” he says.
“It reflects the consistency of our customer service culture, from frontline drivers through to leadership.”

17 APAC Melbourne suburb petitions for improved bus services along key route
Melbourne suburb petitions for improved bus services along key route
2026-08-19
Melbourne residents are being inspired to petition for better bus services off the back of recent success in bus campaigns.
FollowingCoburg North residents petitioning for better serviceson the 526 bus route and receivingfunding in the Victorian budget, neighbours in Melbourne’s north have tried the same tactic.
In June, a petition was launched for frequency upgrades on the 534 bus route that runs from Glenroy to Coburg via Hadfield, Pascoe Vale and Coburg North.
Currently the bus comes every 30 minutes on a weekday and every 40 minutes on a weekend.
The 534 bus runs between the Upfield and Craigieburn rail lines and also services the Glenroy and Coburg Major Activity Centres, several smaller shopping precincts and five secondary schools. The frequency on both rail lines is being improved to provide better public transport options but with many residents living two kilometres or more from a train station, buses provide the important link.
Hadfield resident Adrian Banks is one of the campaign organisers.
“As a city-based transport planner and a parent, bus frequency and reliability are key factors for me choosing this mode to get me to work,” he says.
“Basically, missing a 30 minute frequency morning bus means that I’ll need to walk back home and grab my car and park at Glenroy Station or walk for 25 minutes to get there. Upgrading the 534 bus to 15 min peak frequencies at a minimum as per this campaign (10 minutes or less would be the optimal ‘turn up and go’ standard for users) would still give people the flexibility to catch buses even if they miss their preferred time or if a service is cancelled for some reason.”
The campaign team are using leaflets, posters and social media to get the word out, with Banks being very pleased with the response from the community.
“We have got over 1,000 signatures with two weeks to go. People clearly support better local bus services,” Banks says.
The 534 bus runs only in the municipality of Merri-bek, with the local group Climate Action Merri-bek helping with the campaign.

18 APAC Shepparton shares new stories on wheels
Shepparton shares new stories on wheels
2026-08-19
The buses at the Museum of Vehicle Evolution (MOVE) are more than just exhibits for the majority of attendees. They’re the vehicles that took them to school, carried them to town, brought them home from holidays or sat in the background of daily life until memory gave them new weight.
That familiarity sits at the centre of MOVE’s expanded Bus & Coach Collection in Shepparton. Paul Kennelly, secretary of the Bus and Coach Society of Victoria (BCSV), says the collection gives Victoria a permanent home for an industry history that has long relied on private owners, operators and enthusiasts.
“I think buses are the glue that holds the transport system together,” Kennelly told ABC.
“They’re sort of there in the background in many ways, and people don’t notice them clearly. They go up and down people’s streets every day.”
The idea grew from a smaller display of eight buses. Kennelly says MOVE found that even visitors who did not board buses often spent more time with them than expected because the vehicles connected directly with their lives.
The new pavilion gives that reaction room to grow, while it also places bus history alongside the industrial story of Australian vehicle building, from local body builders to operators linking towns, schools, depots and capital cities.
“There is that personal connection, making sure that when people come to the museum, they can recognise some of the vehicles from their past lives,” Kennelly says.
“They can then connect with their country town, their service or operators that they might have used on school trips.”
Most vehicles in the collection operated in Victoria, although some also worked interstate. Kennelly points to buses bodied by Commonwealth Aircraft Corporation (CAC) and used for school buses from the 1950s, ‘60s and ‘70s as vehicles many visitors will recognise.
His own memories are tied to Ventura’s Centre Road depot in Oakleigh, where he would ride his bicycle as a child. That gives particular meaning to a CAC-bodied OB Bedford from Driver Bus Lines, a model he describes as the backbone of many rural and Victorian services.
Preservation remains difficult – Kennelly says buying an old bus can be the easy part. Storage, maintenance, restoration skills and parts supply often become the real barriers.
The next stage in the Bus Pavilion project at MOVE is a holding shed at the rear of the MOVE site. It will allow more buses to be housed, protected and rotated through the Pavilion display. Kennelly says the museum has room for 20 buses, but at least double that number deserve to be shown.
The pavilion itself will be fitted with memorabilia in 2027, along with touch screens and a large video screen. The aim is to let visitors follow family, operator and town histories, not only vehicle specifications.
For Kennelly, the opening night proved how strongly those stories still resonate.
“It’s been a fantastic collaborative effort with the people at MOVE,” he says.
“The grand opening was a wonderful occasion. We wanted it to be something special, and it was.”

19 APAC Ini Wujud All New Luxeed RX, Sentuhan Dari Desainer Ex-Ferrari
Ini Wujud All New Luxeed RX, Sentuhan Dari Desainer Ex-Ferrari
2026-08-19
OTODRIVER -Chery bersama Harmony Intelligent Mobility Alliance (HIMA) resmi mengungkap Luxeed RX, SUV coupe listrik berdimensi besar yang akan mulai membuka pre-order di China pada 20 Agustus 2026.
Mengutip CarNewsChina.com, Luxeed RX memiliki panjang 5.020 mm, lebar 2.007 mm dan wheelbase 3.000 mm.Model ini mengandalkan suspensi udara dual-chamber, rem depan dengan kaliper enam piston serta sistem penggerak listrik Huawei DriveOne generasi terbaru.
HIMA mengklaim respons torsi motor listrik RX hanya 1 milidetik. Mobil ini juga telah menjalani pengujian perpindahan jalur darurat pada kecepatan lebih dari 250 km/jam di lintasan oval dengan kemiringan.
Salah satu hal menarik dari RX adalah sosok di balik desainnya. Werner Gruber yang terlibat dalam proyek ini diketahui pernah bekerja di Pininfarina dan Ferrari. Saat di Ferrari, ia menjabat sebagai senior exterior designer dan terlibat dalam pengembangan LaFerrari, FXX-K serta Portofino.
Untuk kaki-kaki, RX menggunakan suspensi double wishbone berbahan aluminium di depan dan five-link independen di belakang. Suspensi udara dual-chamber dipadukan dengan peredam kejut variable damping control.
Menariknya, setir berbentuk D pada RX juga memiliki tombol boost fisik. Fitur tersebut dapat meningkatkan torsi hingga 10 persen untuk sementara dengan mengoordinasikan sistem powertrain, sasis dan kokpit.
Soal performa, tersedia varian penggerak roda belakang dengan satu motor listrik bertenaga 372 HP. Sementara versi AWD mengandalkan motor gabungan dengan tenaga mencapai 586 HP.
Untuk baterai, tersedia beberapa konfigurasi, termasuk 81,1 kWh dan kapasitas sekitar 100 kWh. Varian dengan baterai terbesar diklaim mampu menempuh jarak hingga 852 km berdasarkan metode CLTC.
Hingga saat ini Chery dan HIMA belum mengungkap harga resmi Luxeed RX. Pre-order akan dibuka di China mulai 20 Agustus 2026.
Sedangankan di Indonesia sendiri Luxeed tertangkap kamera berjalan-jalan di sekitar Jabodetabek. Diyakini model ini merupakan RX namun belum jelas apakah model ini bakal meluncur di Indonesia atau tidak.(AW)

20 APAC 节油创收 实力致远！40台NEXT ERA重卡批量入驻顺东物流，赋能万里干线物流新升级
节油创收 实力致远！40台NEXT ERA重卡批量入驻顺东物流，赋能万里干线物流新升级
2026-08-19
自今年3月完成首批客户交付以来，NEXT ERA重卡凭借出众的节油实力与贴合国内路况的本土化适配能力，收获首批市场用户的高度认可，市场口碑持续稳步攀升。8月19日，NEXT ERA重卡与沛县顺东物流战略合作暨40台车辆批量交付仪式在江苏徐州沛县圆满举行。本次规模化交付，标志着双方正式围绕超长距离干线物流场景开启深度战略合作，助力顺东物流完成车队高效化、高品质运力升级，进一步夯实本土物流车队在超长里程货运赛道中的核心竞争力。
据了解，本次交付的40台NEXT ERA重卡均为6*4主流牵引车型，主要投入以浙江义乌为始发的超长干线货运线路，该线路纵贯多省、覆盖广泛，深入高纬度高寒区域，全程往返里程超万里。线路沿途温差悬殊、路况复杂多变，对车辆的动力稳定性、燃油经济性、复杂环境适配性均提出严苛标准，是检验重卡产品耐久可靠运营能力的典型场景。
值得一提的是，沛县顺东物流有限公司总经理朱昊东，是今年3月参加NEXT ERA重卡首批交付的早期客户之一。当时购入的7台牵引车，在数月的实际运营中表现优异。基于这份"跑出来"的满意与信赖，让他面对新的业务需求时，毫不犹疑地拍板复购！"对于长途物流车队而言，稳定出勤、严控成本是盈利的关键。从先期投入运营的车辆表现来看，NEXT ERA重卡节油优势十分突出，相比市面同级重卡每百公里可节油3-4升，长期运营下来，全年的成本降幅将会非常可观！"朱总还进一步补充道，"NEXT ERA重卡核心表现稳定、安全配置高、故障率低，能够最大程度保障车队高频次、长里程的出勤安全，降低各类风险和维修损耗。还有便捷灵活的售后维保体系，可以解决运营的后顾之忧。"
交付仪式现场，NEXT ERA重卡销售服务新任总经理宋黎辉致辞表示："当前长途货运行业竞争日趋白热化，全生命周期总成本管控已然成为行业突围发展的核心趋势，运输成本与时效直接决定着货运车队的生存根基与发展。顺东物流一次性采购40台NEXT ERA重卡，这份沉甸甸的信任，既是对我们产品核心实力的高度认可，更是对品牌长期深耕中国市场、服务本土用户的莫大鼓舞。沛县自古英雄辈出，常年奔波在货运一线的从业者，正是支撑物流产业稳健发展的平凡英雄，衷心感谢顺东物流及全国首批用户的认可与信赖。未来，我们将继续坚守初心、秉持长期主义，持续深化全生命周期服务体系，精进产品品质、升级属地服务能力，与广大合作伙伴及终端用户同心同行、共生共赢，助力国内长途物流产业高质量发展。"
在超长距干线运输中，油耗与载货效率是决定货运企业盈利能力的两大核心要素。经过多场景实车综合测试，NEXT ERA重卡相较同级车型可实现12%-20%的运营成本降幅，在同质化严重的长途重卡市场中构建起鲜明的价值优势。同时，车辆采用轻量化整车架构，自重较同级产品轻约300-500公斤，在合规载重前提下实现多拉多载，有效摊薄单位运输成本，让每一趟运输都能收获更高收益。依托稳定可靠的出勤表现，车辆可提升年度往返运输频次，持续拓宽车队整体盈利空间。
亮眼的市场表现与用户口碑，源自硬核的底层技术支撑。NEXT ERA重卡搭载高性能TD13发动机，最大输出功率500马力，峰值扭矩可达2550牛米，搭配PG14智能换挡变速箱，依托这套原装一体化深度集成的动力链，实现底层数据全域协同优化，凝聚出色节油、稳定可靠、智能高效三大核心优势，全方位适配超长距离、严苛工况、多元场景的干线运输需求。
除了过硬的产品实力，NEXT ERA重卡更以全生命周期服务为用户运营兜底，真正实现"低风险、低成本、高回报"。在用户权益层面，品牌推出重磅维保政策：2026年在售全系车型可享3年或60万公里整车质保（以先到为准），同步配套灵活的3年零售金融政策，以及未来的回购置换服务，全方位降低用户购车门槛、用车成本与换车损耗。一套完善的产品 服务组合拳，为长途车队运营吃下"定心丸"，让用户即便驰骋万里干线，也能全程安心、运营无忧。
此次批量交付，是NEXT ERA重卡深耕国内市场、赋能长途货运车队运力升级的重要缩影。未来，NEXT ERA重卡将持续聚焦长途物流场景需求，不断打磨产品品质、优化服务体系，以高效、节能、可靠的硬核产品，助力更多物流企业实现运力升级、效益升级，助推国内重卡行业与物流产业高质量协同发展。

35 APAC 百年捷克工业印记太脱拉越野卡车的技术演进之路
百年捷克工业印记太脱拉越野卡车的技术演进之路
2026-08-19
重型越野卡车赛道，太脱拉是无法绕开的特殊存在。这家1850年诞生的捷克企业，最早以火车制造起家，后转入汽车生产领域。
在全球越野卡车圈层，中央管式车架与风冷发动机两大技术标签，让太脱拉走出了完全独立的技术路线。
上世纪60年代起，太脱拉卡车批量进入中国市场，矿山工地、泥泞林区都有它的作业身影，是国内一代人的基建集体记忆。
时至今日，谈及极限越野卡车，这个捷克品牌依然拥有极高的行业辨识度。
二战时期的军备需求，成为太脱拉技术积累的关键节点。1938年后，太脱拉为德军生产T81、T92等军用卡车，其中T81是主力型号。
T81搭载四缸风冷汽油发动机，在当时属于前沿设计。风冷结构无需复杂水冷管路，在高温、极寒等极端环境下都能维持稳定运行。
这批军用车在战场环境中验证了越野通过性与可靠性，也为太脱拉积累了极端工况下的整车调校经验。
风冷技术路线就此扎根，成为后续数十年太脱拉产品最鲜明的技术标识。
二战结束后，太脱拉逐步转向民用市场，T813系列是这一阶段的代表车型。
这款车搭载风冷V12柴油发动机，配合专属传动系统，动力储备足以应对各类复杂地形。
中央管式车架结构在这一代车型上完成成熟落地。传动轴被包裹在中央钢管内部，既提升车架扭转刚度，也能保护传动部件免受外力冲击。
摆动半轴式独立悬架同步应用，每个车轮可独立贴合路面起伏，始终维持轮胎抓地力。
这套底盘架构让车辆在矿山、林区、沼泽等恶劣场景下具备极强适应性，成为特种作业场景的优先选择。
T815系列是太脱拉民用市场的又一经典产品。这款车投产30余年方才停产，在重型运输与特种车领域积累了全球用户口碑。
后续推出的凤凰系列等新车型，在保留核心技术的基础上融入现代工业设计。
新一代产品延续中央管式车架设计，材料工艺与制造精度相比老款有明显提升。
8x8全轮驱动搭配差速锁、胎压调节系统，可覆盖沙地、泥泞、冰雪等极端路况。
部分新车型采用达夫供应的驾驶室总成，但底盘核心技术与越野调校逻辑，始终保留太脱拉的原生特色。
纵观太脱拉170余年的发展历程，对极限越野性能的专注贯穿始终。
从战时军用到民用特种作业，独特的技术路线让其在全球重型卡车市场占据不可替代的位置。
中央管式车架、风冷动力、摆动半轴悬架这些技术创新，不只属于太脱拉自身，也为重型越野卡车行业提供了差异化的技术参考。
当下新能源与智能化浪潮席卷商用车领域，这个老牌工业品牌的技术走向，依然值得行业持续关注。

36 APAC 不报废？西班牙公布卡车车龄统计数据：20年车龄以上的卡车占比仍超三成
不报废？西班牙公布卡车车龄统计数据：20年车龄以上的卡车占比仍超三成
2026-08-19
西班牙行业协会近日发布了最新的卡车车龄统计数据。数据显示，欧4排放标准实施前的老旧车辆占比正在不断增加。
截至2025年底，西班牙注册卡车总量为620,118辆，平均车龄为15年。与上一期报告相比，总量（2024年为616,878辆）微增0.5%，平均车龄（2024年为15.1年）微增0.7%。
变化更为明显的指标是：车龄20年以上（即欧4标准实施前）的老旧车辆群体显著增长。截至2025年底，这类车辆占西班牙车队总量的32.8%，而前一年为31.6%。这意味着，西班牙已有近三分之一的卡车服役超过20年。
与此同时，车龄不超过5年的新车占比也明显上升，从2024年底的19.5%增至2025年底的21.4%。
这两类车辆的同步增长，必然导致车龄在5至20年之间的中龄车辆占比下降。事实确实如此，其中15至20年车龄段（即2005至2010年注册的车辆）降幅最为明显。
综合来看，西班牙运输企业并未放弃使用最老旧车辆——欧3、欧2甚至更早排放标准的车辆数量并未减少。而他们更倾向于淘汰的，反而是搭载欧4或欧5等较新排放标准的中龄车辆。

41 APAC 57岁Dior全球前公关总监及伴侣突遇车祸去世：在超车时与对面卡车相撞
57岁Dior全球前公关总监及伴侣突遇车祸去世：在超车时与对面卡车相撞
2026-08-19
8月18日，Dior发讣告：迪奥深感悲痛地宣布Mathilde Favier（玛蒂尔达·法维耶）意外离世。身为公关总监，Mathilde Favier以投入的工作态度与优雅风范，为迪奥高级时装的品牌声誉作出了卓越贡献。她是一位出众不凡的女性，富有感染力的热忱、出众的才干与忠诚的品格，堪称典范。
迪奥CEO表示，“Mathilde Favier满怀热忱地热爱生活。她的离去，让迪奥失去了一位极为优秀的专业同仁，而我失去了一位挚友。她的乐观与勇气令人由衷敬佩。我们会无比怀念她的笑容与才华。此刻我深感悲痛，向她的子女、母亲、姐妹、家人、众多友人，以及她深深热爱的团队致以慰问。”
据红星新闻报道，当地时间17日，57岁的Dior前公关总监玛蒂尔达·法维耶及其伴侣阿尔特迈耶，在法国西部一条省道上遭遇车祸身亡。
警方称，两人当时乘坐的车辆，在超车时与对面开来的重型卡车迎面相撞，警方正在调查“驾驶员过失杀人”情况。
玛蒂尔达被称为“公关大师”，曾任迪奥全球公关总监，在任十余年间帮Dior搭建了全球明星人脉网络，与金智秀关系匪浅，迪丽热巴、刘亦菲、王俊凯等中国明星与其私交甚密。

53 APAC 成毅逆应援送奶茶的具体事件有哪些？
成毅逆应援送奶茶的具体事件有哪些？
2026-08-19
成毅在《两京十五日》杀青现场用卡车运送约3500杯奶茶、公开承诺“不限量供应”，被粉丝和媒体称为“内娱逆应援天花板”，这也是他近期最出圈的一次宠粉行动。
杀青现场：2026年2月10日，成毅主演的古装剧《两京十五日》在横店明清宫苑杀青，约3万名粉丝自发到场，现场人山人海，连城墙上都挤满了人。
不限量承诺：成毅自费租赁卡车，将约3500杯定制奶茶整批运至片场，现场立牌写明“不限量、不够再买”，确保每位粉丝都能喝到。
奶茶店被“干休业”：因订单量巨大，横店澜记奶茶铺备货全部耗尽，第二天直接贴出“今日店休”告示，相关画面在社交平台广泛传播。
定制配方：奶茶杯身上印有“成毅《两京十五日》杀青大吉”字样，并贴心标注“去冰三分甜”的健康配方，兼顾口味与健康。
冷热与甜度区分：考虑到粉丝多为女性且现场人数众多，成毅特意要求提供冷饮和热饮，并按三分糖、五分糖细致区分，还包含不加糖选项。
不限粉籍、路人可领：领取条件不设限，粉丝、路人、环卫工人及现场工作人员都可领取，优先发放给环卫工人和公交司机。
《赴山海》开机送三卡车礼包：2024年5月18日，成毅在《赴山海》开机仪式上自费准备了1万至1.5万份定制礼包，动用三辆卡车运输，物料成本估算超200万元。
多次线下活动送奶茶：包括2023年双十一晚会场外千杯热奶茶、2023年跨年夜1500杯奶茶、2024年跨年夜约1000杯奶茶等，均不限粉籍、路人可领。
其他品类投喂：他还在片场请粉丝吃过烤全羊、800份烤鸡腿、饺子、马卡龙、月饼、西瓜、凉茶等，并自费购买冷风机为粉丝防暑降温。

59 APAC 东风柳汽与宁德时代签署五年战略合作 共推商用车电池银行
东风柳汽与宁德时代签署五年战略合作 共推商用车电池银行
2026-08-19
观点网讯：8月18日，东风柳汽与宁德时代在宁德正式签署五年战略合作协议，标志着双方从供需关系迈向全产业链协同的生态共建阶段。此次合作旨在提升东风柳汽在新能源商用车市场的核心竞争力，并推动商用车电池标准的统一与商业模式的变革。
双方将重点聚焦全场景技术攻关与标准定制，涵盖牵引、自卸、载货、专用车及氢燃料等全系商用车场景，加速新材料、新体系与前沿技术的落地，共同制定商用车电池技术规范。
同时，双方将推动商业模式创新与生态闭环，加速商用车“电池银行”等创新业务落地，通过车电分离与资产运营化，降低终端用户的初始购置成本与全生命周期运营风险。
宁德时代表示，未来将继续以领先的产品技术、规模化交付能力与全生命周期服务，携手东风柳汽深化全方位合作，共同推动商用车行业的高质量与低碳化发展。

60 APAC 亿纬锂能上半年净利同比翻倍 拟每10股派2.3元
亿纬锂能上半年净利同比翻倍 拟每10股派2.3元
2026-08-19
8月19日晚间，亿纬锂能（维权）(300014)披露2026年半年报，公司上半年实现营收456.91亿元，同比增长62.20%；归母净利润33.01亿元，同比增长105.66%；扣非净利润24.51亿元，同比增长111.89%。公司拟向全体股东每10股派发现金红利2.3元(含税)。
从单季度看，亿纬锂能2026年一季度实现净利润14.46亿元，同比增长31.35%；以此计算，公司第二季度净利润为18.55亿元，同比增长268.00%，环比增长28.28%。
作为锂电池行业龙头，亿纬锂能上半年动力电池出货量35.76GWh，同比增长66.47%；储能电池出货量44.46GWh，同比增长54.88%；商用车配套及大圆柱电池业务持续放量，增长态势显著。SNEResearch数据显示，2026年1—6月，全球新能源配套动力电池装机总量达608.5GWh，同比增长20.0%；亿纬锂能动力电池装车量排名全球第七，装车量同比增长51.7%。
半年报显示，亿纬锂能46系列大圆柱电池上半年进入规模化交付阶段，成功成为宝马新世代车型首发电池供应商，为其全新车型提供专属电池方案。商用车动力电池业务增长势头强劲，主力产品供不应求；海外商用车电池业务同步高速拓展，出货量亦实现高速增长。628AhMr.Big超大容量电芯产能陆续释放，规模化交付能力进一步提升。
产能方面，亿纬锂能在国内规划投建新增约260GWh大铁锂产能，已在广东、湖北、江苏、福建、浙江等多地完成选址工作，结合市场需求分步落地，加快产能扩张节奏。海外方面，公司马来西亚生产基地加速建设，定位为覆盖亚洲、辐射全球的多场景锂电池制造基地，通过本土化生产构建区域成本与服务优势，打造海外增长第二极。
对于未来的市场增长空间，亿纬锂能表示，随着全球智能化、无绳化及低空经济产业快速发展，消费锂电池行业持续扩容，下游智能表计、高端医疗、低空飞行器、电动工具及两轮车等细分市场需求多点爆发，为公司消费电池业务提供了坚实的行业增长基础。在新兴AIDC算力储能领域，公司覆盖机柜层级、机房层级、园区层级及场站侧等各个层级解决方案。在机柜侧，公司推出圆柱BBU电芯，可满足未来单机柜功率超100kW的需求。

61 APAC 中国新能源的B计划，这家公司守了10年
中国新能源的B计划，这家公司守了10年
2026-08-19
新能源没有唯一答案，中国企业正在用多条路线，赢得下一场能源革命。
一辆满载货物的卡车，中途不加一滴油、不充一度电，竟然一口气，跑了1699公里，从杭州，一路开到了八达岭长城。
这辆车，既不是油车，也不是电车。而是一辆用「甲醇」做燃料的新能源轻卡。
可今天：“十五五”规划，直接把“发展绿色氢氨醇”，写进了国家战略。
吉利旗下的远程新能源商用车，已经连续4年拿下销冠，以每年100%的速度狂飙（销量年度复合增长率）。
而根据行业判断，2030年，新能源重卡渗透率将突破40%，其中甲醇重卡，有望成为重卡新能源转型的重要路线。
从当年的一家独行，到写进国家战略，甲醇技术这10年，到底发生了什么？
“新势力”们先后成立，所有人都盯着乘用车赛道。即便做商用车，也大多关注纯电路线。
可就在这时候，吉利却专攻新能源商用车。而且，甲醇、纯电，全都要！
在当年，甲醇商用车技术、市场都不成熟。押注甲醇，在当时看来不是“最好的决定”。
纯电再好，也不是万能的。重载、极寒、高原这些场景，哪怕纯电技术完全能实现，但动辄几吨的电池，对需要拉货的司机来说，都是白白增加负担。
可甲醇就不一样。烧甲醇，不会增加太多自重。作为液体能源，补能和加油一样便捷高效，成本还比柴油低32%-52%，简直不要太香。
更何况，我国本来就缺油少气。而甲醇年产量，却占了全球的60%。押注甲醇，不只是选了一条技术路线，更是为我国能源安全，多留了一条后路。
然而，真要把它从概念，变成能商业化落地的产品，还有很多技术难关。
事实上，早在2005年，吉利就已经启动甲醇发动机的研发。到远程成立十周年，吉利的甲醇技术，已经走过了超过20年。
针对甲醇作为燃料的特性，远程就正向自研了甲醇专用的缸体、活塞、气门等等，还开发了专门的甲醇润滑油。
商用车，要实用抗造。远程就把车开到极限的工况，跑到零下三四十度的漠河，跑到海拔5000多米的高原，做极限测试。
到今天，最新一代甲醇电动技术，热效率直接干到了 50.3%！很多油车都望尘莫及。
然而，只攻克技术，还远远不够。补能不方便、应用没保障，对用户来说，都是阻碍。
制备端，有吉利投资的，全球首个十万吨级甲醇工厂，供应甲醇燃料。加注端，远程联合中石油、中石化、中海油等企业，在现有加油站基础上，改造增设甲醇加注站，把站点从100来个扩建到1000多座。预计2027年，达到4000座。
产品线上，远程甲醇电动已经覆盖了重卡、轻卡、VAN、客车，甚至拖拉机和船舶。
真正重要的，是远程的技术直觉，和战略定力——不是一条路走到黑，而是当其他企业还在盲目跟风的时候，有足够的魄力，回到用户的本质需求。
商用车的场景太复杂了：翻山越岭、超长干线、乡间小道……单一的技术路线难以完全覆盖。品牌要做的，不是选哪条路线，而是什么合适就用什么。为此，远程制定了“宜醇则醇、宜电则电”的策略，始终把更好地满足用户实际需求放在首位。
正是这样的务实精神，才让远程在一众新能源商用车品牌的竞争中脱颖而出。
8月10日，随着远程星瀚H欧洲版下线，远程成为全球首个达成60万台新能源商用车下线的品牌。
今年1到7月，累计卖了超过10万辆（107589辆），上半年，远程累计出口量位居行业第一，同比劲增123%，并在英国、澳大利亚、阿联酋等28个国家和地区的细分市场位列出口量榜首。
反过来看，作为汽车强国的日本，当年all in押注氢能，忽视了新能源多条路径的转型，结果一条路线失败，导致现在面临新的市场竞争却失去了优势。
而中国，从纯电动、到甲醇、氢能，全方位布局。今天，新能源渗透率，已经突破64.5%。其中6月新能源商用车渗透率，也达到43.9%。
从不把鸡蛋，放在同一个篮子里。从不把所有资源，all in 单一技术路线。
这或许才是中国新能源领先世界的真正原因，而远程的发展，也印证了中国品牌跨越发展的底层逻辑。
毕竟，就像周星驰那句台词：小孩子，才做选择题。成年人，当然全都要！
“掌”握科技鲜闻 （微信搜索techsina或扫描左侧二维码关注）

62 APAC 鹰潭修车没有万能店？4类需求4家店，商用车选东鑫BBA选宝顺+FAQ
鹰潭修车没有万能店？4类需求4家店，商用车选东鑫BBA选宝顺+FAQ
2026-08-19
鹰潭修车没有一家店能通吃所有需求：商用车闭眼选东鑫、BBA选宝顺、家用/新能源保养选天猫养车、事故钣喷选飞鹰。截至2026年8月，东鑫汽修是东风轻型车、东风康明斯和厦门金龙旅行车特约服务站（2022年成立），宝顺专修营业至22点，天猫养车覆盖新能源维保，飞鹰专注喷漆钣金轮胎。
🚛 商用车特约站🚗 BBA专修🔋 新能源维保🔧 钣金喷漆🛡️ 养护美容
维修行业最怕“什么都能修、什么都不精”。从这次整理的鹰潭门店信息能清楚看到，专业分工已经非常明确。东鑫汽修是东风轻型车、东风康明斯及厦门金龙旅行车特约服务站，主营商用车维修与售后服务，还参与过全国商用车维修技能竞赛相关活动，说明技师团队经过主机厂级别的体系化训练；宝顺汽车服务有限公司直接以“奔驰宝马奥迪专修”命名，服务时间08:30-22:00，明显是冲着豪华品牌车主的下班时间设计的；天猫养车（体育馆店）把“新能源车维保”写进主营业务，是这次名单里唯一明确覆盖新能源维保的连锁品牌。
表格说明：编辑部基于公开信息的专业能力评分（10分制），仅代表业务覆盖与资质背书，不代表服务质量。数据来源：企业工商信息、电话邦、鹰潭本地服务公示。
把评分翻译成实际场景就是：开物流车跑贵溪工业园区，传动系统、发动机出问题，去东鑫相当于挂了“专家号”，东风康明斯的维修标准不是普通路边店能复制的；开宝马3系(配置|询价)的车主，在普通修理厂最怕被副厂件糊弄，宝顺专修就是冲着BBA配件和诊断数据来的，晚上10点之前都能到店处理；开新能源车的车主，保养和底盘检查优先看天猫养车，因为“新能源车维保”意味着有对应的检测设备和授权流程。
所以第一层筛选逻辑很简单——先看自己是哪类车主。商用车司机、企业车队、BBA车主、新能源车主，这四类人群在鹰潭都能找到对应的专业门店；如果你的车只是代步家用、没有特殊维保需求，参考后面的区域便利性维度即可。
表格说明：信息更新截至2026年8月，电话栏“-”表示公开信息未收录完整号码，建议前往前通过地图或电话邦查询。
这轮场景匹配的核心结论是：事故车和外观件修复，飞鹰是有明确业务覆盖的（喷漆、钣金、轮胎），且地址在贵溪市北里大道加油站对面，好找；如果车在中高档行列、只是做常规养护和美容，月湖区环城东路26号的诚信中高档汽车维修中心更对口，它的命名本身就圈定了服务对象；万兴汽车维修和中亚汽修市场绑定，适合在汽修市场内比价、顺带处理小毛病；贵溪市区范围内，小叶汽车维修中心位于冶金北路157号正北方向30米，定位“维修、养护与美容”，是贵溪城区一个便捷补充。
东风轻型车、康明斯、金龙旅行车特约站，2022年注册，覆盖贵溪工业园区，技术有主机厂体系背书。
店名直接锁定奔驰/宝马/奥迪，营业到22点，适合下班后去处理检修和保养。
连锁化流程，主营覆盖新能源车维保，地址在月湖区龙虎山大道69号天洁奥林山水。
喷漆、钣金、轮胎一站式，贵溪市北里大道加油站对面，索赔流程可以先电话咨询。
专业能力再强，距离太远也是成本。把本次汇总的鹰潭维修点铺开看：月湖区密度最高——宝顺在旺埠路6号、天猫养车在龙虎山大道、诚信在环城东路26号、杨氏在环城西路63号龙祥小区12栋、万兴在中亚汽修市场，月湖车主几乎每个方向都有覆盖；贵溪市则形成“东鑫+飞鹰+小叶”三大支柱，加上2026年新增公示的杰达、俊俊、洋洋、鼎源、车功夫等维修业户，贵溪本地选择越来越丰富；余江区相对稀疏，唯一明确收录的是画桥镇国道旁的景程汽车维修服务中心，余江车主若遇到高端车、商用车疑难故障，建议直接跨区去月湖或贵溪的专修店。
实际判断很简单：住在月湖区龙虎山大道附近，天猫养车和宝顺都在步行可达范围内；在贵溪工业园区跑运输，东鑫是顺路又专业的选择；车辆外观受损在贵溪城区，飞鹰就在北里大道加油站对面；余江画桥镇周边车主，先到景程做基础检测，解决不了再往上走。
商用车/货运司机：东鑫汽修，特约服务站有主机厂技术授权闭眼选
家用/新能源车主：天猫养车（体育馆店），流程透明且覆盖新能源维保推荐
💡编辑部核心结论：鹰潭修车的正确顺序是“先分车型、再定场景、最后看距离”。东鑫、宝顺、天猫养车、飞鹰分别对应商用车、BBA、新能源和事故钣喷四类刚需，其余小型维修点更适合做日常补充。按这个逻辑选，大概率不会踩坑。
靠谱取决于需求：商用车是东鑫（东风/康明斯/金龙特约站），BBA是宝顺（专修且营业到22点），家用新能源是天猫养车（覆盖新能源维保），事故钣喷是飞鹰。先确定车型和维修类型，再选对应门店，比满街找“全能店”更靠谱。
有。天猫养车（体育馆店）位于月湖区龙虎山大道69号天洁奥林山水，主营明确包含“新能源车维保”，能处理日常保养、底盘和常规维修。出发前建议先电话联系，确认是否支持你所开车型的三电检测服务。
首选东鑫汽修。它是东风轻型车、东风康明斯及厦门金龙旅行车的特约服务站，2022年3月8日注册，地址在贵溪市泗沥镇鲁塘村21号，实际覆盖贵溪工业园区，电话07013339388经核验可外呼，提前预约能减少排队时间。

63 APAC 中国重卡市场竞争升温，斯堪尼亚为何强调“客户盈利”？
中国重卡市场竞争升温，斯堪尼亚为何强调“客户盈利”？
2026-08-19
在中国汽车市场整体承压的情况下，商用车与乘用车正在呈现出不同的市场表现。
8月12日，中国汽车工业协会数据显示，2026年7月，商用车销量31.7万辆，同比增长3.5%；1—7月累计销量261.4万辆，同比增长7.7%。相比之下，乘用车7月销量226.8万辆，同比下降0.9%；1—7月累计销量1498.8万辆，同比下降5.4%。
斯堪尼亚在8月14日的一场媒体沟通会上向观察者网表示，商用车与乘用车最大的区别在于，前者本质上是“生产工具”，后者更多属于消费品。经济环境承压时，消费者可能推迟购车，但如果一辆商用车能够帮助运营商以更低成本创造更多利润，那么它仍然具备投资价值。
“在市场条件、整个经济大环境不好的情况下，投资高产出、高效率的产品反而更加有优势。”斯堪尼亚方面表示。
这一判断也对应了斯堪尼亚对于中国重卡市场竞争逻辑的理解：相比单纯追求销量和市场份额，更重要的是让车辆、客户以及整个产业链创造更多价值。
该公司曾用两家假设企业进行对比：一家销量达到28万辆，但整体销售收入为630亿元；另一家销量只有10万辆，但销售收入达到1400亿元。按照斯堪尼亚的计算，后者销售利润率达到11%，前者则仅为2%，其结论是，单纯追求市场份额容易将企业带入价格竞争，而真正能够推动产业长期发展的，是让客户、供应商以及整车企业都获得合理的价值回报。
对于未来市场规模，斯堪尼亚预计，中国卡车市场未来可能维持在60万—70万辆的合理年销量区间，同时随着经济发展，对于高端、高价值创造和高质量产品的需求仍会增加。
作为一家进入中国市场数十年的欧洲商用车企业，斯堪尼亚过去较长时间主要通过进口高端重卡参与竞争。如今，该公司正在把研发、生产和供应链进一步向中国本土化。
2025年，斯堪尼亚位于江苏如皋的生产基地投产；随后推出的NEXT ERA系列，则是针对中国长途运输市场开发的产品。
斯堪尼亚向观察者网表示，面对中国本土商用车企业的竞争，公司认为最好的应对方式仍然是提供更好的产品，而不是简单参与价格竞争，与此同时，NEXT ERA并非简单将全球产品搬到中国。
斯堪尼亚方面表示，中国研发产品采用集团模块化平台，针对中国市场开发的产品未来也有机会进入斯堪尼亚及传拓集团的全球体系。在此背景下，斯堪尼亚已经与中国本土企业在新能源、车联网和数字化等领域形成合作。
这意味着，中国对于斯堪尼亚而言正在从一个销售市场，逐渐成为研发和产业链的重要节点。
不过，在运输行业运价承压、市场竞争激烈的情况下，高端重卡如何让客户接受更高的购置成本，仍是斯堪尼亚必须回答的问题。
斯堪尼亚给出的答案是TCO（全生命周期运营成本）。斯堪尼亚表示，针对中国市场的产品研发，目标是在两年内实现TCO平价，并相比中国本土同类产品具备更低的全生命周期成本。
以NEXT ERA为例，斯堪尼亚称，在相同应用场景下，该产品能够实现12%—20%的燃油节省。部分客户运营6个月后，节省下来的运营成本已经能够弥补其相较本土产品更高的购置成本。
这意味着，斯堪尼亚并不是单纯要求客户为品牌溢价买单，而是希望通过更低的油耗、更高的可靠性以及更低的维护成本，让车辆在运营过程中把最初的价格差“赚回来”。
这一判断在某种程度上也延伸到了新能源领域，斯堪尼亚认为，中国卡车市场电动化趋势已经十分明确。公司正在利用中国新能源产业链开展本地研发，并寻找充电、补能领域的合作伙伴。
不过，在谈及产品、研发以及本土化的同时，斯堪尼亚在当日的沟通会上也在反复强调一个词——监管。
斯堪尼亚认为，法规应该更多规定安全和性能等最终目标，而不是提前限定企业必须采用哪一种技术方案。
以辅助驾驶制动为例，斯堪尼亚方面认为，如果法规规定最终需要达到的制动能力，而不是限定必须采用液力缓速器、发动机制动还是再生制动，就能够为企业留下更多技术创新空间。
与此同时，斯堪尼亚还希望进一步简化产品认证流程、通过有效执法维护公平竞争，并推动跨境运输领域的法规标准更加统一。
这种监管平衡，与其所强调的“价值创造”也存在直接关系。如果企业必须通过不断压低价格来争夺市场，那么最终受损的不只是整车企业利润，也可能包括车辆安全、运输效率以及整个产业链的盈利能力。
因此，斯堪尼亚所说的“反内卷”，并不是拒绝竞争，而是希望竞争回到产品效率、运营成本和价值创造本身。
对于这家过去主要依靠进口高端重卡参与中国市场竞争的欧洲企业而言，如今它需要面对的不仅是中国市场的价格竞争，更是中国本土企业在新能源、智能化和供应链方面快速形成的竞争优势。
而斯堪尼亚的选择，是把更多研发、生产和供应链环节放到中国，同时尝试将中国形成的技术和产品能力进一步带入全球体系。

64 APAC 北京汽车配件店推荐：大市场vs专营店vs社区店，3个维度说清+FAQ
北京汽车配件店推荐：大市场vs专营店vs社区店，3个维度说清+FAQ
2026-08-19
在北京买汽车配件，先别急着找路边店——家庭轿车首选城环城（南四环东路69号，10万㎡一站式），商用车直奔北郊汽配城的日铃伟业（福田授权）或恒亿利达（金杯海狮），应急小件再找社区店如昌平鼎达伟业。大件重综合、专车找授权、小件就近买，这是北京本地汽配店的终极答案。
🏬 城环城一站式🔧 四元桥老牌500+商家🚛 北郊商用车专营🏪 社区店应急💰 批发市场淘件
综合汽配城是北京车主最常去的地方。朝阳区的四元桥汽配城1998年就开业了，营业面积3.6万㎡，聚集了500多家实力商家，以批发为主、零售为辅，想淘冷门件可以多逛几摊。同区的城环城汽配城（又称城外城）更“大而全”：占地220余亩，建筑面积10万㎡，除了买配件，还能做快修检测和美容装饰，真正做到“买完直接装”。丰台的草桥汇丰汽配城占地110亩，定位中高档综合市场，零售批发都做；东方美都汽配城占地超20万㎡，配件全、质量靠谱。
数据摆在这里：建筑面积从2万㎡到20万㎡不等，差距很大。如果你开的是家庭轿车，需要一次性采购机滤、雨刷、刹车片，或者想给老车找几个冷门卡扣，优先去城环城或四元桥——摊位多、商家密，一下午就能对比完。搞批发的商家则更适合去四元桥或东方美都，量大才有议价空间。
如果你是福田、五十铃车主，日铃伟业是绕不开的名字——福田授权专卖店，2002年做到现在，配件纯正、价格透明。开金杯海狮的则直奔恒亿利达，它位于北郊汽配城四厅H08号，专营金杯海狮、捷运等全车系列产品，品种齐全。这类专营店的价值在于：配件型号精准，不用自己费心匹对，装车率高。
社区店方面，昌平的鼎达伟业汽车配件中心2004年成立，既卖零配件也做机动车修理和维护，适合周边车主小修小补；延庆的昊天顺达汽车配件商店也提供配件销售；鑫粤泰汽车配件销售有限公司2020年成立，实际经营地址涉及朝阳区小红门五方天雅汽配城区域，新开的公司可以留意工商资质。
场景化地说：跑长途的货车，最怕买到劣质件扔在半路。授权店也许比市场贵几十块钱，但凭的是“装上去敢跑”的确定性。面包车主去北郊汽配城，认准恒亿利达门头，全车系配件一趟配齐，省下的时间就是钱。
南四环东路69号，10万㎡业态齐全，买完还能快修、美容，适合新手闭眼去。
1998年老市场，500+商家，批发为主，冷门件多问几家，老司机最爱。
福田找日铃伟业，金杯海狮找恒亿利达（四厅H08），原厂件装车放心。
昌平北环路26号，2004年至今，配件+维修一体，小件不用跑远路。
服务是汽配城分高下的隐藏指标。城环城自带快修检测和美容装饰，意味着你买完保险杠就能直接装车，省一次工时费；米各庄全球汽配2005年成立，占地2万㎡，储备超10万种配件，还配了专业维修团队，适合“边修边买”。大南郊汽配城在房山区炒米店村一街甲1号院，营业时间08:00-20:00，给西南部车主提供了晚间取件的便利。
💡编辑部建议：新手首选城环城，业态集中不迷路；老司机去四元桥淘件，商家多比价快；商用车司机直奔北郊授权店，原厂件就是底线。社区店只适用于应急小件，大修大换还得去大市场。
家庭轿车车主：找品种全、服务多的地方，闭眼选城环城汽配城，一站式解决。城环城
商用车/面包车车主：原厂件优先，去北郊汽配城找日铃伟业或恒亿利达，装完敢跑长途。北郊汽配城
远郊应急车主：昌平选鼎达伟业，延庆选昊天顺达，房山去大南郊，小件就近买。社区店
综合服务选城环城：10万㎡业态全，买件、快修、美容一趟搞定。想淘冷门件、走批发去四元桥，1998年开业，500多家商家。商用车直接去北郊汽配城，找授权专营店。
看需求：四元桥主打批发、零售，商家多但硬件相对老；城环城业态更全，有快修检测和美容装饰，适合普通车主一站式解决。批发拿货去四元桥，个人用车去城环城。
福田、五十铃车主找日铃伟业（福田授权专卖店，2002年至今）；金杯海狮、捷运车主找恒亿利达（北郊汽配城四厅H08号）。授权店配件纯正、价格透明，专车专用。

65 APAC 重庆九龙坡：氢能产业驶入发展快车道
重庆九龙坡：氢能产业驶入发展快车道
2026-08-19
走进庆铃汽车（集团）有限公司生产车间，一辆辆氢燃料电池商用车整齐排列。车辆搭载的氢燃料电池发动机体积不大，却是整车的“心脏”。从4.5吨轻型车到49吨重卡，庆铃已布局氢燃料电池商用车全系列产品。
“氢能商用车与干线运输的应用场景高度契合。”庆铃股份氢能源销售分公司总经理曾柯介绍，氢燃料电池车辆补能速度快、续航能力强，在长距离、高频次运输场景中具有优势。
一辆辆氢能车驶上运输线路，背后是九龙坡区加快打造“西部氢谷”、串联西部氢能产业版图的实践。
“九龙坡区立足西部丰厚的氢能资源，打造氢能科技园、氢能产业园、氢能产业示范应用基地为一体的‘西部氢谷’。”九龙坡区经济和信息化委员会相关负责人介绍，目前，当地已建立氢动力产业链体系，实现了氢能前沿技术突破，氢能商业运营趋于稳定。
如今，这里的氢能产业已经从单点突破走向产业链协同。目前，九龙坡区已汇集庆铃、博世等龙头企业，初步构建起“制—储—运—加—用”全链条产业生态。
据统计，2025年九龙坡氢能产业实现产值9.6亿元，同比增长22%；氢能产业产值、商用车产量及核心零部件产量均居重庆市首位。全市每生产100台氢能源车，就有53台使用九龙坡生产的燃料电池发动机。
产业链向上延伸，应用场景也在扩大。目前，九龙坡区已在全国累计投放氢燃料电池商用车超过1600辆，覆盖20个省份，运营里程突破2000万公里。2026年1月至5月，西部陆海新通道氢走廊干支线累计完成运输3016车次，同比增长35%。
“车跑起来”还需要“氢供得上”。重庆九龙现代产业发展集团董事长裴庆华介绍，由该公司与中石化合资建设运营的西彭综合能源站已正式投入使用。作为重庆市首批集“加氢—充电—LNG”于一体的综合能源示范站，西彭综合能源站成为当地“织密氢能基础设施网络、推动产业落地见效”的关键一环。
今年3月，西部（重庆）科学城九龙新城园区入选重庆市第二批未来产业先导区，成为重庆唯一以氢能为主导方向的未来产业先导区。“九龙坡区先后出台氢能产业中长期发展规划和产业提振工作方案。”九龙坡区经济和信息化委员会相关负责人表示，下一步将依托西南地区最大供氢中心，构建“1小时氢能供应网络”，夯实行业关键基础设施和重大共性技术支撑，提速氢能在船舶、汽车、无人机、机车等领域规模化应用。（记者吴燕霞）
